# 考研英语·生词本

## ⚡ 多次没记住的单词

> <!-- 这里只放单词本身，不加其他信息。你记不住的词我放这里 -->
>
`contagious` `reconcile` `contingent` `contemplate` `aggregate` `heir` `reservoir` `treaty` `wipe` `denounce` `fling` `salient` `surplus` `hospitality` `controversial` `strap` `discrete` `municipal` `suffice` `ruthless` `decimal` `formation` `dual` `plagiarism` `intersection` `radius` `equator` `haul` `consult` `knit` `grip` `gown` `wicked` `territory` `ascribe` `seminar` `penalty` `shuttle` `mortgage` `vulgar` `elicit` `clause` `convene` `mingle` `fraud` `epoch` `league` `famine` `furious` `exquisite` `resume` `reverse` `aerial` `roundabout` `wreck` `gang` `counsel` `considerable` `likewise` `grief` `bronze` `ascertain` `adjacent` `debut` `beware` `courtesy` `radiant` `thermometer` `limp` `feeble` `huddle` `lane` `fore` `dispute` `indignant` `snap` `vigorous` `plunge` `conscientious` `crude` `wagon` `apparatus` `stack` `litter` `transient` `integral` `naive` `drawback` `allegiance` `booth` `porcelain` `nuisance` `thrift` `repel` `toil` `tow` `overhear` `burglar` `escort` `skull` `stipulate` `cohesive` `exaggerate` `merit` `brace` `thrust` `turnover` `hypocrisy` `wage` `imminent` `eradicate` `reciprocal` `undermine` `impart` `doze` `militant` `thermal` `barren` `cereal` `curb` `commence` `hoist` `kidnap` `contrive` `sly` `spacious` `outlet` `parasite` `inflict` `notion` `sow` `spectacle` `reproach` `rifle` `dismay` `perplex` `deficit` `lease` `excursion` `dividend` `cupboard` `liner` `imperative` `module` `seam` `priest` `razor` `audit` `forum` `handicap` `soar`

---

| 日期 | 单词 | 音标 | 释义 | 常用搭配 | 例句 |
|:---:|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| 2026-05-25 | vicinity | /vəˈsɪnəti/ | n. 附近，邻近地区 | in the vicinity (of) 在…附近<br>in close vicinity 在邻近 | There are several hotels in the **vicinity** of the train station. （火车站附近有几家酒店。）<br>The police searched the **vicinity** for clues. （警方在附近搜寻线索。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：neighborhood（更具体指居住区）· **易混**：vicinity vs neighborhood（vicinity 强调地理上的"附近"范围，neighborhood 特指住宅街区）|
| | surplus | /ˈsɜːrpləs/ | n. 过剩，剩余；盈余<br>adj. 多余的，过剩的 | trade surplus 贸易顺差<br>budget surplus 预算盈余<br>surplus value 剩余价值 | The country has a large trade **surplus** with its neighbors. （该国对邻国有巨额的贸易顺差。）<br>Any **surplus** funds will be invested in new projects. （任何多余的资金都将投入新项目。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：excess（常有过量之意）· **反义**：deficiency/shortage · **易混**：surplus vs excess（surplus 偏"剩余"，excess 偏"超额/过度"）|
| | ruthless | /ˈruːθləs/ | adj. 无情的，残忍的；<br>不留情面的，坚决的 | ruthless competition 残酷竞争<br>ruthless efficiency 不留情面的高效<br>ruthless dictator 残忍的独裁者 | The **ruthless** dictator suppressed all opposition. （那个残忍的独裁者镇压了一切反对力量。）<br>We need a **ruthless** focus on our goals. （我们需要对目标保持毫不留情的专注。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：merciless（冷酷无情的）· **反义**：merciful/compassionate · **易混**：ruthless vs merciless（ruthless 更强调为达目的不择手段）|
| | unanimous | /juˈnænɪməs/ | adj. 一致的，<br>全体同意的 | unanimous decision 一致决定<br>unanimous approval 一致通过<br>be unanimous in (doing) sth 在…方面意见一致 | The jury reached a **unanimous** verdict. （陪审团达成了一致裁决。）<br>They were **unanimous** in their support for the proposal. （他们一致支持该提案。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：unified（统一的）· **反义**：divided/dissenting · **易混**：unanimous vs uniform（unanimous 指"意见一致"，uniform 指"外形统一"）|
| | suffice | /səˈfaɪs/ | v. 足够，足以<br>（使）满足 | suffice (it) to say (that) … 只需说…就够了<br>suffice for 满足…的需要 | **Suffice** it to say that the project was a complete success. （只需说这个项目非常成功就够了。）<br>One example will **suffice** to illustrate the point. （一个例子就足以说明这一点。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：be enough（更口语化）· **易混**：suffice vs satisfy（suffice 指"足够"，satisfy 指"使满足"）|
| | gown | /ɡaʊn/ | n. 长袍，礼服；<br>（手术）罩衣 | evening gown 晚礼服<br>graduation gown 毕业礼服<br>hospital gown 病号服 | She wore a stunning evening **gown** to the party. （她穿了一件惊艳的晚礼服去参加派对。）<br>The doctor put on a surgical **gown** before the operation. （医生在手术前穿上了手术罩衣。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：robe（长袍，通常指家居或仪式用）· **易混**：gown vs dress（gown 指正式长袍/礼服，dress 泛指连衣裙）|
| | outlet | /ˈaʊtlet/ | n. 出口，排水口；<br>专卖店；<br>发泄途径 | power outlet 电源插座<br>retail outlet 零售店<br>outlet for sth …的发泄途径 | Make sure the washing machine is plugged into a power **outlet**. （确保洗衣机已插入电源插座。）<br>Running is a good **outlet** for stress. （跑步是释放压力的好方式。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：vent（发泄口，更口语化）· **易混**：outlet vs exit（outlet 强调"排放口/出路"，exit 强调"出口/离开"）|
| | appendix | /əˈpendɪks/ | n. 附录；<br>阑尾 | in the appendix 在附录中<br>see appendix 见附录<br>ruptured appendix 阑尾破裂 | Please refer to the **appendix** for detailed data. （详细数据请参考附录。）<br>He had his **appendix** removed last year. （他去年切除了阑尾。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：appendix vs attachment（appendix 书"附录"，医学"阑尾"；attachment 指邮件附件或情感依恋）|
| | hound | /haʊnd/ | n. 猎犬<br>v. 追踪，追赶；<br>纠缠 | follow hounds 骑马带着猎犬打猎<br>be hounded by 被…纠缠/追赶<br>hound sb out of 把某人赶出… | The reporters **hounded** the celebrity for comments. （记者们缠着那位名人要评论。）<br>He was **hounded** out of office by the scandal. （他因丑闻被赶下了台。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：chase(追逐) / hound纠缠追, hunt打猎搜索|
| | dwell | /dwel/ | v. 居住；<br>细想，详述 | dwell on/upon 老是想着；详述<br>dwell in 居住在… | Don't **dwell** on past mistakes. （不要总纠结于过去的错误。）<br>He **dwelt** in a small village for years. （他在一个小村庄住了多年。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：reside(居住) / dwell on含纠结意, ponder中性深思|
| | owe | /oʊ/ | v. 欠（债、情等）；<br>归功于 | owe sth to sb 欠某人某物<br>owe it to oneself 认为自己有必要<br>owe...to... 把…归功于 | I **owe** you an apology. （我欠你一个道歉。）<br>She **owes** her success to hard work. （她把成功归功于勤奋工作。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：be indebted to(欠) / repay(反) / owe归功好处, attribute中性归因|
| | cable | /ˈkeɪbl/ | n. 电缆，缆绳；<br>有线电视 | cable car 缆车<br>cable TV 有线电视<br>lay cables 铺设电缆 | The **cable** TV went out during the storm. （暴风雨期间有线电视断了。）<br>They are laying underwater **cables** for internet connection. （他们正在铺设海底互联网电缆。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：wire(电线) / cable粗缆绳, cord细绳|
| | tempt | /tempt/ | v. 引诱，诱惑；<br>吸引 | tempt sb into (doing) sth 引诱某人做某事<br>be tempted to do sth 忍不住想做某事<br>tempt fate/providence 冒险，玩命 | The warm weather **tempted** us to go for a swim. （温暖的天气吸引我们去游泳。）<br>I'm **tempted** to quit my job and travel the world. （我很想辞职去环游世界。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：lure(引诱) / repel(反) / tempt含诱惑做坏事意味|
| | transient | /ˈtrænziənt/ | adj. 短暂的，<br>转瞬即逝的；<br>临时的 | transient population 流动人口<br>transient pleasure 短暂的快乐<br>transient phenomenon 短暂现象 | The beauty of cherry blossoms is **transient**. （樱花之美是短暂的。）<br>He had a series of **transient** jobs after graduation. （毕业后他做过一系列临时工作。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：temporary(暂时) / permanent(反) / transient短暂|
| | loose | /luːs/ | adj. 松的，宽松的；<br>松散的，不精确的；<br>v. 释放，松开 | loose change 零钱<br>loose clothing 宽松的衣服<br>cut loose 摆脱；切断<br>break loose 挣脱出来 | She wore a **loose** dress on a hot summer day. （炎热的夏天她穿了一件宽松的裙子。）<br>The dog broke **loose** from its leash. （那只狗挣脱了狗绳。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：relaxed(放松) / tight(反) / loose松的, lose丢失|
| | cricket | /ˈkrɪkɪt/ | n. 板球；<br>蟋蟀 | play cricket 打板球<br>cricket match 板球比赛<br>cricket bat 板球拍<br>not cricket （英式口语）不公正的 | He is a passionate **cricket** player. （他是一名热情的板球运动员。）<br>The sound of **crickets** filled the summer night. （夏夜里到处是蟋蟀的叫声。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cricket有板球/蟋蟀两个无关意思|
| | proceedings | /prəˈsiːdɪŋz/ | n. 诉讼，法律程序；<br>会议记录，论文集 | legal proceedings 法律诉讼<br>take proceedings 提起诉讼<br>conference proceedings 会议记录/论文集 | The bank started legal **proceedings** against him. （银行对他提起了法律诉讼。）<br>The **proceedings** of the conference will be published next month. （会议论文集将于下个月出版。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：proceedings诉讼/会议录, procedure程序步骤|
| | butcher | /ˈbʊtʃər/ | n. 屠夫，肉贩<br>v. 屠宰；<br>残杀，弄糟 | butcher's (shop) 肉店<br>butcher sb/sth 屠宰；弄糟 | The **butcher** cut the meat into thin slices. （肉贩把肉切成了薄片。）<br>They **butchered** the play in their review. （他们在评论中把这部戏批得一无是处。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：slaughterer(屠夫) / butcher屠宰弄糟, massacre大屠杀|
| | offend | /əˈfend/ | v. 冒犯，得罪；<br>违反，违背 | offend sb 冒犯某人<br>offend against 违反（法律/习俗）<br>be offended by/at 因…而生气 | I'm sorry if I **offended** you. （如果我冒犯了你，我很抱歉。）<br>His actions **offended** against the rules of the school. （他的行为违反了学校的规定。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：insult(侮辱) / please(反) / offend冒犯情感, violate违反规则|
| | loom | /luːm/ | v. 赫然显现；<br>逼近，迫在眉睫<br>n. 织布机 | loom large 显得严重/突出<br>loom ahead 逼近，迫近 | The threat of war **loomed** over the country. （战争的威胁笼罩着这个国家。）<br>Economic crisis is **looming** on the horizon. （经济危机即将来临。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：emerge(浮现) / loom含威胁逼近意, appear中性出现|
| | patrol | /pəˈtroʊl/ | n. 巡逻，巡查<br>v. 巡逻，巡查 | on patrol 在巡逻中<br>police patrol 警察巡逻<br>patrol car 巡逻警车 | Security guards **patrol** the building every night. （保安每晚在大楼巡逻。）<br>The police car is on **patrol** in the neighborhood. （警车正在该社区巡逻。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：patrol巡逻警戒, survey调查勘测|
| | nominal | /ˈnɑːmɪnl/ | adj. 名义上的；<br>象征性的，极小的 | nominal fee 象征性费用<br>nominal value 面值，名义价值<br>nominal head 名义上的领导 | There is only a **nominal** charge for the service. （这项服务只收取象征性的费用。）<br>He is the **nominal** head of the organization. （他是该组织的名义上的领导。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：token(象征性) / actual(反) / nominal名义上, minimal最小量|
| | chew | /tʃuː/ | v. 咀嚼；<br>深思，细想 | chew on/over 仔细考虑，琢磨<br>chew up 嚼碎<br>bite off more than one can chew 贪多嚼不烂 | **Chew** your food thoroughly before swallowing. （吞咽前充分咀嚼食物。）<br>I need some time to **chew** on this problem. （我需要一些时间来琢磨这个问题。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：chew on口语化琢磨, ponder正式深思|
| | oar | /ɔːr/ | n. 桨，橹 | put/stick one's oar in 多管闲事，干涉<br>rest on one's oars 停下来歇口气 | He dipped his **oar** into the water and pulled hard. （他把桨插入水中用力划。）<br>Nobody asked you to put your **oar** in. （没人让你多管闲事。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：oar固定桨, paddle手持桨|
| | heir | /er/ | n. 继承人，后嗣 | heir to the throne 王位继承人<br>legal heir 法定继承人<br>heir apparent 法定继承人 | He is the **heir** to a large fortune. （他是一大笔财产的继承人。）<br>The prince is the **heir** to the throne. （这位王子是王位继承人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：heir继承人, heritage遗产传统|
| | thrust | /θrʌst/ | v. 猛推，猛塞；<br>刺，戳<br>n. 推力；要点，主旨 | thrust sth into 把某物塞进/刺入<br>thrust sb aside 把某人推开<br>the main thrust 核心要点 | He **thrust** his hands into his pockets. （他把双手插进口袋。）<br>The main **thrust** of the argument is that education needs reform. （该论点的核心是教育需要改革。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：push(推) / thrust猛力刺入, shove粗鲁推|
| | merit | /ˈmerɪt/ | n. 优点，价值；<br>功绩<br>v. 值得，应得 | have merit 有价值，有优点<br>merit pay 绩效工资<br>on its/his own merits 根据本身情况 | The plan has considerable **merit**. （这个计划很有价值。）<br>Your suggestion **merits** further discussion. （你的建议值得进一步讨论。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：value(价值) / demerit(反) / merit可作动词值得|
| | throne | /θroʊn/ | n. 宝座，王位 | heir to the throne 王位继承人<br>take the throne 登基，即位<br>the throne 王位，王权 | The prince ascended the **throne** after his father's death. （王子在父亲去世后继承了王位。）<br>Queen Elizabeth II sat on the **throne** for over 70 years. （伊丽莎白二世女王在位超过70年。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：throne王位, crown王冠王权象征|
| | volatile | /ˈvɑːlətl/ | adj. 易变的，<br>反复无常的；<br>（化学）易挥发的 | volatile market 动荡的市场<br>volatile situation 动荡局势<br>volatile temper 暴躁的脾气 | The stock market has been highly **volatile** recently. （最近股市波动很大。）<br>The political situation remains **volatile**. （政治局势仍然动荡不安。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：unstable(不稳定) / stable(反) / volatile易变, vulnerable脆弱|
| | swarm | /swɔːrm/ | n. （昆虫）群，一大群<br>v. 蜂拥，聚集 | a swarm of 一群（昆虫/人）<br>swarm with 挤满，充满<br>swarm into 蜂拥而入 | A **swarm** of bees flew out of the tree. （一群蜜蜂从树上飞了出来。）<br>The beach was **swarming** with tourists. （海滩上挤满了游客。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crowd(拥挤) / swarm蜂拥, flock成群(羊鸟)|
| | pact | /pækt/ | n. 协定，条约，契约 | sign a pact 签署协议<br>peace pact 和平条约<br>mutual defense pact 共同防御条约 | The two countries signed a trade **pact**. （两国签署了一项贸易协定。）<br>They made a **pact** never to reveal the secret. （他们约定决不泄露秘密。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：treaty(条约) / pact可个人间协定, treaty正式国际条约|
| | verse | /vɜːrs/ | n. 诗句，诗节；<br>韵文 | write verse 写诗<br>chapter and verse 详细地；精确出处<br>free verse 自由诗 | She wrote a beautiful **verse** for the occasion. （她为此场合写了一首优美的诗。）<br>He can quote chapter and **verse** on tax law. （他能精确引用税法条文。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：verse诗句, version版本|
| | garage | /ɡəˈrɑːʒ/ | n. 车库；<br>修车厂 | parking garage 停车场<br>garage sale 庭院旧货出售<br>garage door 车库门 | She parked the car in the **garage**. （她把车停在车库里。）<br>I took my car to the **garage** for a repair. （我把车送去修车厂修理。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：garage车库修车厂, parking lot停车场|
| | alleviate | /əˈliːvieɪt/ | v. 减轻，缓解，<br>缓和 | alleviate pain 减轻疼痛<br>alleviate poverty 缓解贫困<br>alleviate symptoms 缓解症状 | The medicine helped **alleviate** her pain. （药物帮助减轻了她的疼痛。）<br>New policies were introduced to **alleviate** poverty. （出台了新政策来缓解贫困。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：relieve(缓解) / alleviate减轻痛苦贫困, release释放|
| | municipal | /mjuːˈnɪsɪpl/ | adj. 市的，<br>市政的 | municipal government 市政府<br>municipal council 市议会<br>municipal services 市政服务 | The **municipal** government plans to build a new library. （市政府计划建造一座新图书馆。）<br>She works for the **municipal** water company. （她在市政自来水公司工作。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：municipal市政, metropolitan大都市|
| | pipe | /paɪp/ | n. 管子，管道；<br>烟斗<br>v. 用管道输送 | water pipe 水管<br>gas pipe 煤气管<br>pipe dream 空想，幻想 | A burst water **pipe** flooded the basement. （一根爆裂的水管淹没了地下室。）<br>Oil is **piped** across the desert. （石油通过管道穿越沙漠输送。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：tube(管子) / pipe管道, tube软管试管|
| | treaty | /ˈtriːti/ | n. 条约，协定 | sign a treaty 签署条约<br>peace treaty 和平条约<br>under the treaty 根据该条约 | The two nations signed a peace **treaty**. （两国签署了和平条约。）<br>The **treaty** came into effect last month. （该条约上个月生效了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：agreement(协议) / treaty国家间条约, pact协定|
| | legislation | /ˌledʒɪsˈleɪʃn/ | n. 立法，法规，<br>法律 | pass legislation 通过立法<br>introduce legislation 提出立法<br>environmental legislation 环境法规 | The government plans to introduce new **legislation** on data privacy. （政府计划提出新的数据隐私立法。）<br>Congress passed **legislation** to protect the environment. （国会通过了保护环境的法规。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：law(法律) / legislation立法法规, legislature立法机关|
| | wheat | /wiːt/ | n. 小麦 | wheat field 麦田<br>wheat flour 小麦面粉<br>separate the wheat from the chaff 区分优劣 | The farmer planted **wheat** in the field. （农民在田里种了小麦。）<br>We need to separate the **wheat** from the chaff in the candidates. （我们需要从候选人中甄别优劣。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：wheat小麦, corn玉米, barley大麦|
| | hospitality | /ˌhɑːspɪˈtæləti/ | n. 好客，殷勤；<br>（款待客人的）食物饮料 | hospitality industry 酒店餐饮业<br>hospitality suite 迎宾套房<br>show hospitality 表示殷勤好客 | Thank you for your warm **hospitality**. （感谢您的热情款待。）<br>She works in the **hospitality** industry. （她在酒店餐饮行业工作。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hospitality热情好客, hospital医院|
| | governor | /ˈɡʌvərnər/ | n. 州长；<br>总督；理事 | state governor 州长<br>board of governors 理事会<br>governor general 总督 | The **governor** signed the bill into law. （州长签署了该法案使其成为法律。）<br>He was elected **governor** of California. （他当选为加利福尼亚州州长。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：governor州长总督, government政府|
| | vapour | /ˈveɪpər/ | n. 蒸气，水汽<br>（美式拼写：vapor） | water vapour 水蒸气<br>vapour trail （飞机的）拉烟<br>mercury vapour 汞蒸气 | Water **vapour** in the air condenses into clouds. （空气中的水蒸气凝结成云。）<br>The **vapour** rose from the hot cup of tea. （热气从热茶杯中升起。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：steam(蒸汽) / vapour蒸气泛指, steam水蒸气|
| | marble | /ˈmɑːrbl/ | n. 大理石；<br>（玻璃）弹珠 | marble statue 大理石雕像<br>marble floor 大理石地板<br>lose one's marbles 失去理智 | The palace has a beautiful **marble** floor. （宫殿有漂亮的大理石地板。）<br>The children were playing **marbles** in the yard. （孩子们在院子里玩弹珠。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：marble大理石, marvel奇迹|

| | bypass | /ˈbaɪpæs/ | n. 旁路，支路；<br>（心脏）搭桥手术<br>v. 绕过，避开 | bypass surgery 搭桥手术<br>bypass the system 绕过系统<br>a bypass road 旁路，支路 | He had heart **bypass** surgery last year. （他去年做了心脏搭桥手术。）<br>We managed to **bypass** the traffic jam by taking a side road. （我们走小路绕过了交通堵塞。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bypass绕过旁路, pass通过|

| | drift | /drɪft/ | v. 漂，漂流；<br>无目的地移动<br>n. 漂流；趋势，大意 | drift apart 渐渐疏远<br>drift off 渐渐入睡<br>get the drift 明白大意 | The boat **drifted** slowly down the river. （小船顺着河流缓缓漂流。）<br>They used to be close friends, but they have **drifted** apart over the years. （他们曾是密友，但多年来逐渐疏远了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：float(漂流) / drift漂移, draft草稿/ draft通风|

| | utmost | /ˈʌtmoʊst/ | adj. 最大的，极度的<br>n. 最大限度，极限 | of the utmost importance 至关重要的<br>do one's utmost 竭尽全力<br>with utmost care 极其小心地 | This matter is of the **utmost** importance. （这件事至关重要。）<br>He did his **utmost** to help the victims. （他竭尽全力帮助受害者。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：maximum(最大) / utmost最大的, utter完全的|
| | heap | /hiːp/ | n.（凌乱的）一堆；<br>大量，许多<br>v. 堆积，堆放 | a heap of 一堆；大量<br>in a heap 堆成一堆<br>heap praise on 对…大加赞扬 | There was a **heap** of dirty clothes on the floor. （地板上有一堆脏衣服。）<br>She **heaped** praise on her students. （她对学生们大加赞扬。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：pile(堆) / heap杂乱一堆, stack整齐一摞|
| | brace | /breɪs/ | v. 使防备；支撑，加固<br>n. 支架，托架；<br>（pl.）牙箍 | brace oneself for 为…做好准备<br>brace up 振作起来<br>neck brace 颈托 | We **braced** ourselves for the upcoming storm. （我们为即将到来的暴风雨做好了准备。）<br>The wall needs to be **braced** with steel beams. （这面墙需要用钢梁加固。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：support(支撑) / brace支架/支撑, embrace拥抱|
| | likelihood | /ˈlaɪklihʊd/ | n. 可能性，可能 | in all likelihood 十有八九，极可能<br>reduce the likelihood 降低可能性<br>increase the likelihood 增加可能性 | In all **likelihood**, the meeting will be postponed. （会议极有可能会被推迟。）<br>The **likelihood** of success is very high. （成功的可能性非常高。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：probability(可能性) / likelihood可能性, likely可能的|
| | contemplate | /ˈkɑːntəmpleɪt/ | v. 沉思，深思熟虑；<br>考虑，打算；<br>凝视 | contemplate doing sth 考虑做某事<br>contemplate the meaning of life 思索人生的意义<br>contemplate one's future 思考未来 | She sat quietly, **contemplating** her next move. （她静静地坐着，思考下一步该怎么做。）<br>He **contemplated** changing his career path. （他考虑过改变职业道路。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ponder(深思) / contemplate凝视深思, consider考虑|
| | stewardess | /ˈstuːərdəs/ | n. 女乘务员，空姐 | air stewardess 空姐<br>stewardess uniform 空姐制服<br>chief stewardess 乘务长 | The **stewardess** served drinks to the passengers. （空姐为乘客端上饮料。）<br>She worked as a **stewardess** for five years before becoming a pilot. （她在成为飞行员之前当了五年空姐。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：stewardess空姐, steward男乘务员|
| | crow | /kroʊ/ | n. 乌鸦<br>v.（公鸡）啼叫；<br>自鸣得意 | as the crow flies 成直线地，直线距离<br>crow about/over 为…自鸣得意<br>crow's feet 鱼尾纹 | It's only five miles away as the **crow** flies. （直线距离只有五英里。）<br>He was **crowing** about his victory all day. （他一整天都在为自己的胜利自鸣得意。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crow乌鸦/啼叫, cow母牛, crown王冠|
| | thrust (第2次) | /θrʌst/ | v. 猛推，猛塞；<br>刺，戳<br>n. 推力；要点，主旨 | thrust sth into 把某物塞进/刺入<br>thrust sb aside 把某人推开<br>the main thrust 核心要点 | He **thrust** his hands into his pockets. （他把双手插进口袋。）<br>The main **thrust** of the argument is that education needs reform. （该论点的核心是教育需要改革。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：push(推) / thrust猛力刺入, shove粗鲁推|
| | chunk | /tʃʌŋk/ | n. 厚块，大块；<br>大量 | a chunk of 一大块<br>chunk of time 大量时间<br>break into chunks 分成小块 | She cut the bread into thick **chunks**. （她把面包切成厚块。）<br>A big **chunk** of his salary goes to rent. （他工资的一大块都花在房租上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：lump(块) / chunk大块, chuck扔|
| | formulate | /ˈfɔːrmjuleɪt/ | v. 制定，规划；<br>确切表达 | formulate a plan 制定计划<br>formulate a policy 制定政策<br>formulate an idea 表达想法 | The committee met to **formulate** a new strategy. （委员会开会制定新战略。）<br>He struggled to **formulate** his thoughts clearly. （他努力把自己的想法清晰表达出来。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：devise(设计) / formulate制定/表达, implement实施|
| | expedition | /ˌekspəˈdɪʃn/ | n. 远征，探险；<br>远征队 | go on an expedition 去探险<br>scientific expedition 科学考察<br>mountaineering expedition 登山探险 | They organized an **expedition** to the South Pole. （他们组织了一支南极探险队。）<br>The **expedition** lasted three months in the rainforest. （这次科考在雨林中持续了三个月。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：exploration(探险) / expedition远征, exhibition展览|
| | invisible | /ɪnˈvɪzəbl/ | adj. 看不见的，无形的 | invisible to the naked eye 肉眼看不见的<br>invisible barrier 无形障碍<br>invisible hand 无形之手（经济） | Bacteria are **invisible** to the naked eye. （细菌肉眼看不见。）<br>There is an **invisible** connection between them. （他们之间有一种无形的联系。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hidden(隐藏的) / visible(反) / invisible看不见的, invincible无敌的|
| | ebb | /eb/ | n. 退潮，衰退<br>v. 退潮；衰退，减退 | ebb and flow 涨落，兴衰<br>at a low ebb 处于低潮<br>ebb away 逐渐消退 | His enthusiasm began to **ebb** away. （他的热情开始消退。）<br>Business is at a low **ebb** this season. （这个季节生意处于低潮。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：recede(退去) / flow(反) / ebb退潮衰退, bud发芽|
| | defer | /dɪˈfɜːr/ | v. 推迟，延期；<br>听从，服从 | defer a decision 推迟决定<br>defer payment 延期付款<br>defer to 听从，遵从 | The meeting was **deferred** until next week. （会议被推迟到下周。）<br>I always **defer** to her judgment on these matters. （在这些事情上我总是听从她的判断。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：postpone(推迟) / defer to听取遵从, differ不同|
| | scope | /skoʊp/ | n. 范围，领域；<br>机会，余地 | within the scope of 在…范围内<br>beyond the scope of 超出…范围<br>scope for 有…的余地 | This topic is beyond the **scope** of this book. （这个主题超出了本书的范围。）<br>There is plenty of **scope** for improvement. （还有很大的改进余地。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：range(范围) / scope余地/范围, cope应对|
| | lane | /leɪn/ | n. 车道；小路，小巷；<br>航道 | bus lane 公交车道<br>country lane 乡间小路<br>fast lane 快车道<br>shipping lane 航线 | Stay in the right **lane** when driving slowly. （慢速行驶时保持在右车道。）<br>They walked hand in hand down a narrow country **lane**. （他们手牵手走在一条狭窄的乡间小路上。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：path(小路) / lane车道小巷, line线/线路|
| | shave | /ʃeɪv/ | v. 剃，刮（胡须等）<br>n. 刮脸 | shave off 剃掉，刮掉<br>have a shave 刮脸<br>close shave 侥幸脱险 | He **shaves** every morning before work. （他每天早上上班前刮胡子。）<br>That was a close **shave** — the car almost hit us! （真是千钧一发——那辆车差点撞到我们！） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：trim(修剪) / shave刮胡子, shove推|
| | blunder | /ˈblʌndər/ | n. 愚蠢的错误，大错<br>v. 犯大错；跌跌撞撞地走 | make a blunder 犯大错<br>blunder into 误入，无意陷入<br>tactical blunder 战术失误 | He made a terrible **blunder** by sending the email to the wrong person. （他犯了个大错，把邮件发错了人。）<br>I **blundered** into the wrong meeting room. （我跌跌撞撞走进了错误的会议室。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mistake(错误) / blunder愚蠢大错, blunder vs blunder(大错vs踉跄)|
| | vague | /veɪɡ/ | adj. 模糊的，含糊的；<br>不明确的 | vague idea 模糊的想法<br>vague memory 模糊的记忆<br>vague answer 含糊的回答 | He gave a rather **vague** answer to the question. （他对这个问题给出了相当含糊的回答。）<br>I have only a **vague** memory of my childhood. （我对童年只有模糊的记忆。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ambiguous(模糊) / specific(反) / vague含糊不清, vogue时尚|
| | liable | /ˈlaɪəbl/ | adj. 有责任的，有义务的；<br>易于…的；<br>可能受…影响的 | liable for 对…有责任的<br>liable to do sth 易于做某事<br>hold sb liable 追究责任 | The company is **liable** for any damage caused. （公司对造成的任何损失负有责任。）<br>He is **liable** to lose his temper when tired. （他累的时候容易发脾气。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：responsible(负责) / liable有责任/易于, reliable可靠的|
| | ratio | /ˈreɪʃioʊ/ | n. 比率，比例 | ratio of A to B A与B的比例<br>at a ratio of 以…的比例<br>student-teacher ratio 师生比 | The **ratio** of men to women in the company is 3:1. （公司里男女比例为3:1。）<br>A high student-teacher **ratio** affects teaching quality. （过高的师生比会影响教学质量。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：proportion(比例) / ratio比率(数学), rate速率/费率|
| | paradigm | /ˈpærədaɪm/ | n. 范例，典范；<br>范式 | paradigm shift 范式转变<br>new paradigm 新范式<br>set a paradigm 树立典范 | The internet has brought about a **paradigm** shift in how we communicate. （互联网带来了沟通方式的范式转变。）<br>This research offers a new **paradigm** for understanding cancer. （这项研究为理解癌症提供了新范式。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：model(典范) / paradigm范式, paradise天堂|
| | portrait | /ˈpɔːrtrət/ | n. 肖像，画像；<br>描述 | portrait of …的肖像<br>self-portrait 自画像<br>family portrait 全家福<br>paint a portrait 画肖像 | The artist painted a beautiful **portrait** of the queen. （画家为女王画了一幅美丽的肖像。）<br>His novel is a vivid **portrait** of life in rural China. （他的小说生动描绘了中国的乡村生活。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：painting(画像) / portra肖像, portray描绘(动词)|
| | physician | /fɪˈzɪʃn/ | n. 医生，内科医生 | chief physician 主任医师<br>attending physician 主治医生<br>consult a physician 咨询医生<br>family physician 家庭医生 | You should consult a **physician** if the symptoms persist. （如果症状持续，你应该去看医生。）<br>She is a respected **physician** at the city hospital. （她是市医院一位受人尊敬的医生。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：doctor(医生) / physician内科医生, surgeon外科医生|
| | descendant | /dɪˈsendənt/ | n. 后代，子孙；<br>派生物 | descendant of …的后代<br>direct descendant 直系后代<br>leave descendants 留下后代 | He is a **descendant** of a famous poet. （他是一位著名诗人的后代。）<br>The tradition has been passed down through generations of **descendants**. （这个传统通过后代代代相传。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：offspring(后代) / descendant子孙, ancestor祖先(反义)|
| | discrete | /dɪˈskriːt/ | adj. 分离的，离散的；<br>各别的 | discrete units 独立单元<br>discrete categories 离散类别<br>discrete steps 分步步骤 | The project was divided into several **discrete** stages. （该项目被分为几个独立的阶段。）<br>These are two **discrete** problems and should be treated separately. （这是两个互不相干的问题，应该分开处理。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：separate(分离) / discrete离散的, discreet谨慎的(拼写极像!)|
| | cargo | /ˈkɑːrɡoʊ/ | n.（船、飞机等装载的）<br>货物 | cargo ship 货船<br>cargo plane 货机<br>carry cargo 运载货物<br>cargo hold 货舱 | The **cargo** ship arrived at the port this morning. （货船今天早上抵达港口。）<br>The plane was carrying a heavy **cargo** of medical supplies. （飞机装载着大量医疗物资。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：freight(货物) / cargo船/机货物, carry搬运|
| | conceal | /kənˈsiːl/ | v. 隐藏，隐瞒；<br>掩盖 | conceal sth from sb 对某人隐瞒某事<br>conceal the truth 隐瞒真相<br>conceal one's identity 隐藏身份 | He tried to **conceal** his disappointment. （他试图掩饰自己的失望。）<br>She **concealed** the truth from her parents. （她对父母隐瞒了真相。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hide(隐藏) / conceal隐瞒, cancel取消|
| | cosy | /ˈkoʊzi/ | adj. 舒适的，惬意的；<br>亲切的 | cosy room 舒适的房间<br>cosy atmosphere 温馨氛围<br>cosy up to 讨好，巴结 | The cabin felt warm and **cosy** with the fire burning. （炉火燃烧着，小屋内温暖舒适。）<br>We had a **cosy** chat over coffee. （我们边喝咖啡边亲切聊天。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：comfortable(舒适) / cosy温馨舒适, cozy美式拼写|
| | notion | /ˈnoʊʃn/ | n. 概念，观念；<br>想法，看法 | have a notion of 对…有概念<br>preconceived notion 先入为主的观念<br>vague notion 模糊的概念 | He has no **notion** of how difficult the task is. （他对这项任务的难度毫无概念。）<br>The **notion** that money brings happiness is questionable. （金钱带来幸福的观念值得商榷。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：idea(概念) / notion观念想法, motion运动|
| 2026-06-07 | presumably | /prɪˈzuːməbli/ | adv. 很可能，大概，想必 | presumably correct 大概是正确的<br>presumably because 可能是因为<br>presumably not 大概不是 | The package has **presumably** been lost in the mail. （包裹大概是在邮寄过程中丢失了。）<br>**Presumably** he didn't receive our message, otherwise he would have replied. （想必他没收到我们的消息，不然他会回复的。） |
| | reconcile | /ˈrekənsaɪl/ | v. 调和，协调；<br>使和解；<br>使一致 | reconcile A with B 使A与B一致/协调<br>be reconciled with 与…和解<br>reconcile oneself to 接受（不愉快的事） | It's hard to **reconcile** his words with his actions. （很难将他的言行统一起来。）<br>She **reconciled** with her brother after years of silence. （在沉默多年后，她和哥哥和解了。）<br>He finally **reconciled** himself to the fact that he would never be a professional athlete. （他最终接受了永远无法成为职业运动员的事实。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：reconcile调和/和解, recognize认出, recommend推荐|
| | aisle | /aɪl/ | n.（教堂、飞机、剧院等座位间的）<br>过道，走道 | aisle seat 靠过道的座位<br>walk down the aisle 结婚（走入教堂过道）<br>in the aisle 在过道里 | Do you prefer a window seat or an **aisle** seat? （您喜欢靠窗还是靠过道的座位？）<br>She walked down the **aisle** in a white wedding dress. （她穿着白色婚纱走过教堂过道。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：aisle过道走廊, isle小岛(同音!)|
| | scorn | /skɔːrn/ | n. 鄙视，轻蔑<br>v. 鄙视，轻蔑；<br>不屑（做某事） | be filled with scorn 充满鄙视<br>pour scorn on 对…嗤之以鼻<br>scorn to do sth 不屑于做某事<br>heap scorn on 大肆嘲笑 | He **scorned** the idea of asking for help. （他不屑于向别人求助。）<br>She poured **scorn** on his proposal. （她对他的提议嗤之以鼻。）<br>Her **scorn** was evident in her tone of voice. （她语气中带着明显的鄙夷。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：contempt(鄙视) / scorn鄙视, score分数(拼写像)|
| | perish | /ˈperɪʃ/ | v. 死亡，暴死；<br>毁坏，消亡 | perish in a fire 在火灾中丧生<br>perish from hunger 饿死<br>perish the thought 别这么想<br>perishable 易腐烂的 | Many soldiers **perished** in the battle. （许多士兵在那场战斗中阵亡。）<br>Ancient civilizations **perished** for various reasons. （古代文明因各种原因而消亡。）<br>**Perish** the thought! I would never do such a thing. （别打这种念头！我绝不会做这种事。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：die(死亡) / perish消亡, cherish珍惜(反义联想)|
| | contingent | /kənˈtɪndʒənt/ | adj. 依条件而定的，取决于<br>n.（军队的）分遣队；<br>代表团 | contingent on/upon 取决于…<br>be contingent on 视…而定<br>a contingent of 一批（代表团/队伍） | Further investment is **contingent** on the company's performance. （进一步的投资取决于公司的业绩。）<br>A **contingent** of troops was sent to the disaster area. （一支分遣队被派往灾区。）<br>Our plans are **contingent** upon the weather. （我们的计划取决于天气。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：conditional(依条件) / contingent取决于, continent大陆|
| | nuisance | /ˈnuːsns/ | n. 讨厌的人（或东西、行为），麻烦事 | make a nuisance of oneself 惹人讨厌<br>public nuisance 妨害公共秩序的行为<br>what a nuisance 真烦人<br>be a nuisance to 对…是个麻烦 | The noise from the construction site is a real **nuisance**. （工地的噪音真的很烦人。）<br>Sorry to be a **nuisance**, but could you help me with this? （抱歉打扰了，能帮我个忙吗？）<br>He made a **nuisance** of himself at the party. （他在聚会上惹人讨厌。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：annoyance(烦恼) / nuisance讨厌事, nursery托儿所|
| | ample | /ˈæmpl/ | adj. 充足的，充裕的；<br>宽敞的 | ample evidence 充分的证据<br>ample time 充足的时间<br>ample opportunity 足够的机会<br>ample space 宽敞的空间 | There is **ample** evidence to support this conclusion. （有充分证据支持这个结论。）<br>You will have **ample** time to review before the exam. （考试前你有充足的时间复习。）<br>The house has **ample** space for a large family. （这房子有足够的空间容纳一个大家庭。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：abundant(充足) / insufficient(反) / ample充裕的, sample样本|
| | cohesive | /koʊˈhiːsɪv/ | adj. 有凝聚力的，团结的；<br>连贯的，衔接的 | cohesive group 有凝聚力的团体<br>cohesive society 有凝聚力的社会<br>cohesive whole 统一的整体<br>cohesive force 凝聚力 | The team needs to be more **cohesive** to succeed. （团队需要更有凝聚力才能成功。）<br>The essay lacks a **cohesive** structure. （这篇文章缺乏连贯的结构。）<br>Language serves as a **cohesive** force in society. （语言在社会中起到凝聚力的作用。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：united(团结) / cohesive有凝聚力的, coherent连贯的|
| | repel | /rɪˈpel/ | v. 击退，驱逐；<br>使厌恶，使反感 | repel an attack 击退进攻<br>repel invaders 击退入侵者<br>be repelled by 被…恶心到/反感<br>water-repellent 防水的 | The army **repelled** the enemy attack. （军队击退了敌人的进攻。）<br>The smell **repelled** everyone in the room. （那股味道让房间里所有人都感到恶心。）<br>This coat has a water-**repellent** finish. （这件外套有防水涂层。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：drive back(击退) / attract(反) / repel击退/使反感, compel强迫|
| | aggregate | /ˈæɡrɪɡət/ | n. 总数，合计<br>adj. 总计的<br>v. 总计，合计；聚集 | in the aggregate 总体上，合计<br>aggregate demand 总需求（经济）<br>aggregate data 汇总数据<br>on aggregate 总计 | In the **aggregate**, the company's profits have increased. （总体来看，公司利润增长了。）<br>The **aggregate** score was 5-3 in their favor. （总分是 5-3，他们获胜。）<br>**Aggregate** demand affects the overall economy. （总需求影响整体经济。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：total(总计) / aggregate总量, congregate聚集|
| | ultraviolet | /ˌʌltrəˈvaɪələt/ | adj. 紫外的，紫外线的<br>n. 紫外线辐射 | ultraviolet rays 紫外线<br>ultraviolet light 紫外光<br>ultraviolet radiation 紫外线辐射<br>protect against ultraviolet 防紫外线 | **Ultraviolet** radiation from the sun can damage your skin. （太阳的紫外线辐射会损伤皮肤。）<br>Sunscreen helps protect against **ultraviolet** rays. （防晒霜有助于防护紫外线。）<br>**Ultraviolet** light is invisible to the human eye. （紫外光是人眼看不见的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ultraviolet紫外线, violet紫罗兰, violate违反|
| | litter | /ˈlɪtər/ | n. 垃圾，废弃物；<br>一窝（幼崽）<br>v. 乱扔垃圾；<br>使杂乱 | litter bin 垃圾桶<br>litter with 使充满（杂乱的东西）<br>no littering 禁止乱扔垃圾<br>a litter of 一窝（小动物） | Please do not **litter** in the park. （请勿在公园乱扔垃圾。）<br>The floor was **littered** with papers. （地板上散落着纸片。）<br>A **litter** of puppies was born last night. （昨晚出生了一窝小狗。）<br>The article is **littered** with spelling mistakes. （那篇文章满是拼写错误。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：garbage(垃圾) / litter乱扔/一窝, little小的(拼写像)|
| | formation | /fɔːrˈmeɪʃn/ | n. 形成，构成；<br>队形，编队；<br>形成物 | rock formation 岩层<br>in formation 排成队形<br>the formation of …的形成<br>word formation 构词法 | The **formation** of new habits takes time. （新习惯的形成需要时间。）<br>The planes flew in **formation**. （飞机编队飞行。）<br>This **rock formation** dates back millions of years. （这个岩层可以追溯到数百万年前。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：structure(结构) / formation形成/编队, information信息|
| | feasible | /ˈfiːzəbl/ | adj. 可行的，行得通的；<br>可能的 | feasible plan 可行的计划<br>feasible solution 可行的解决方案<br>economically feasible 经济上可行的<br>it is feasible to do sth 做…是可行的 | The plan is economically **feasible**. （这个计划在经济上是可行的。）<br>It is **feasible** to complete the project within three months. （三个月内完成这个项目是可行的。）<br>We need to find a **feasible** solution to this problem. （我们需要找到解决这个问题的可行方案。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：practical（更侧重实际效用）· **反义**：infeasible/impossible · **易混**：possible（仅表可能，不强调整体可行性）|
| | decimal | /ˈdesɪml/ | adj. 十进位的，小数的<br>n. 小数 | decimal system 十进制<br>decimal point 小数点<br>decimal place 小数位<br>recurring decimal 循环小数 | The **decimal** system is used worldwide for counting. （十进制在全世界用于计数。）<br>Round the answer to three **decimal** places. （将答案四舍五入到三位小数。）<br>Be careful with the **decimal** point when calculating. （计算时注意小数点。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：decimal十进制/小数, decade十年, decent得体的|
| | strain | /streɪn/ | n. 压力，负担；<br>拉伤，劳损<br>v. 拉紧；<br>使紧张，使不堪承受 | under strain 处于紧张/压力之下<br>strain a muscle 拉伤肌肉<br>put a strain on 给…带来压力<br>eye strain 眼疲劳<br>strain to do sth 尽力做某事 | The lockdown put a huge **strain** on the economy. （封锁给经济带来了巨大压力。）<br>He **strained** his back lifting the heavy box. （他搬重箱子时拉伤了背。）<br>She had to **strain** to hear what they were saying. （她费力去听他们在说什么。）<br>Staring at screens all day causes eye **strain**. （整天盯着屏幕会导致眼疲劳。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：stress（更侧重心理压力）· **易混**：strain vs stress（strain 强调"拉紧"带来的负担，stress 强调"压力"本身）；strict（严格的）|
| | periodical | /ˌpɪriˈɑːdɪkl/ | n. 期刊，杂志<br>adj. 定期的，周期性的 | academic periodical 学术期刊<br>periodical room 期刊阅览室<br>periodical publication 定期出版物<br>weekly/monthly periodical 周刊/月刊 | He subscribed to several academic **periodicals**. （他订阅了几种学术期刊。）<br>The library has a large collection of **periodicals**. （图书馆收藏了大量期刊。）<br>**Periodical** maintenance is required for the equipment. （设备需要定期维护。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：journal(期刊) / periodical期刊, periodic周期性的(形容词)|
| | knit | /nɪt/ | v. 编织，针织；<br>紧密结合 | knit a sweater 织毛衣<br>knit together 紧密结合<br>close-knit 紧密团结的<br>knit one's brows 皱眉 | She **knitted** a scarf for her grandson. （她为孙子织了一条围巾。）<br>They are a **close-knit** family. （他们是一个关系紧密的家庭。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：weave(编织) / knit编织, kit套装/knot结|
| | enclose | /ɪnˈkloʊz/ | v. 围住，包围；<br>随信附上 | enclose sth with 随信附上<br>enclose in 围住，封入<br>enclosed area 封闭区域<br>please find enclosed 随函附上 | Please find **enclosed** a copy of my resume. （随函附上我的简历一份，请查收。）<br>The garden is **enclosed** by a high wall. （花园被高墙围住。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：surround(包围) / enclose附上/围住, disclose揭露|
| | howl | /haʊl/ | v. 嚎叫，怒吼；<br>大声哭喊<br>n. 嚎叫，怒号 | howl with laughter 放声大笑<br>howl in pain 痛苦地嚎叫<br>howl at the moon 对月嚎叫<br>a howl of protest 抗议的怒吼 | The wolves **howled** in the distance. （狼在远处嚎叫。）<br>The wind **howled** through the trees. （风在树林中怒号。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：bark(吠叫) / howl嚎叫, howl vs growl(嚎叫vs咆哮)|
| | rug | /rʌɡ/ | n. 小地毯；垫子 | pull the rug out from under sb 拆某人的台<br>Persian rug 波斯地毯<br>throw rug 小地毯 | She placed a colorful **rug** in the living room. （她在客厅铺了一块彩色小地毯。）<br>He felt like they pulled the **rug** out from under him. （他觉得他们拆了他的台。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：carpet(地毯) / rug小地毯, rug vs carpet(rug小块,carpet整铺)|
| | solidarity | /ˌsɑːlɪˈdærəti/ | n. 团结，团结一致 | in solidarity with 与…团结一致<br>show solidarity 表示团结<br>national solidarity 全国团结 | The workers went on strike in **solidarity** with their colleagues. （工人们罢工以表示对同事的声援。）<br>The community showed great **solidarity** during the crisis. （危机期间，社区展现了极大的团结。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：unity(团结) / solidarity团结一致, solid固体的|
| | consult | /kənˈsʌlt/ | v. 咨询，请教；<br>查阅；商量 | consult a doctor 咨询医生<br>consult with sb 与某人商量<br>consult a dictionary 查词典<br>consult an expert 请教专家 | You should **consult** a lawyer before signing the contract. （签合同前你应该咨询律师。）<br>He **consulted** his notes before answering the question. （回答问题前他查阅了笔记。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：consult咨询查阅, result结果, insult侮辱|
| | precious | /ˈpreʃəs/ | adj. 珍贵的，宝贵的；<br>珍爱的 | precious time 宝贵的时间<br>precious stone 宝石<br>precious memory 珍贵回忆<br>precious resource 宝贵资源 | Time is the most **precious** resource we have. （时间是我们最宝贵的资源。）<br>She kept the **precious** necklace in a safe place. （她把那条珍贵的项链放在安全的地方。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：valuable(宝贵) / precious珍贵的, previous先前的|
| | evacuate | /ɪˈvækjueɪt/ | v. 疏散，撤离；<br>排空 | evacuate from 从…撤离<br>evacuate to 疏散到<br>evacuate the building 疏散大楼<br>evacuate residents 疏散居民 | The building was **evacuated** after the fire alarm went off. （火警响后，大楼被疏散了。）<br>Thousands of people were **evacuated** from the flood zone. （数千人从洪灾区撤离。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：evacuate疏散撤离, evaluate评估, vacuum真空|
| | nickel | /ˈnɪkl/ | n. 镍；<br>（美/加）五分硬币 | nickel and dime 零零碎碎的<br>not worth a nickel 一文不值<br>nickel coin 五分镍币 | The ring is made of **nickel** and silver. （这枚戒指由镍和银制成。）<br>He didn't have a **nickel** to his name. （他身无分文。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：nickel镍/五分硬币, nickel vs nickle(常见拼写错误nickel)|
| | vibrate | /ˈvaɪbreɪt/ | v.（使）振动，（使）颤动；<br>共鸣 | vibrate with 因…而颤动<br>vibrate violently 剧烈振动<br>vibrate in sympathy 产生共鸣 | The phone **vibrated** on the table. （手机在桌子上振动。）<br>The concert hall **vibrated** with the sound of music. （音乐厅随着音乐声而颤动。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：shake(震动) / vibrate振动, vibrant充满活力的|
| | wipe | /waɪp/ | v. 擦，抹，揩；<br>消除<br>n. 擦拭 | wipe out 消灭，彻底摧毁<br>wipe off 擦掉，抹去<br>wipe away 擦去（眼泪等）<br>give sth a wipe 擦拭某物 | She **wiped** the table clean with a cloth. （她用抹布把桌子擦干净。）<br>The tsunami **wiped** out the entire village. （海啸摧毁了整个村庄。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：clean(擦) / wipe擦去, wip vswipe(擦vs滑动)|
| | successor | /səkˈsesər/ | n. 继任者，接替者；<br>后继事物 | successor to 接替…的人/物<br>appoint a successor 任命继任者<br>immediate successor 直接继任者<br>worthy successor 合格的接班人 | The CEO announced his **successor** before retiring. （CEO在退休前宣布了他的继任者。）<br>This model is the **successor** to last year's best-selling phone. （这款产品是去年畅销手机的后续型号。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：follower(继任者) / predecessor前任(反) / successor继承者|
| | imminent | /ˈɪmɪnənt/ | adj. 临近的，即将发生的；<br>迫在眉睫的 | imminent danger 迫在眉睫的危险<br>imminent threat 逼近的威胁<br>imminent arrival 即将到来 | The storm is **imminent** — we need to take shelter now. （暴风雨即将来临——我们必须立刻躲避。）<br>With the deadline **imminent**, everyone worked overtime. （截止日期临近，大家都加班了。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：impending(迫近) / imminent急迫的, prominent突出的|
| | thermal | /ˈθɜːrml/ | adj. 热的，热量的；<br>保暖的 | thermal energy 热能<br>thermal power 火力发电<br>thermal underwear 保暖内衣<br>thermal spring 温泉 | The **thermal** underwear kept him warm in the snow. （保暖内衣让他在雪地里保持温暖。）<br>**Thermal** power stations still provide most of the country's electricity. （火力发电站仍提供该国大部分电力。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：thermal热的/保暖的, thermometer温度计, therapy治疗|
| | closet | /ˈklɑːzət/ | n. 壁橱，衣橱<br>adj. 秘密的，私下的<br>v. 把…关在房间 | walk-in closet 步入式衣橱<br>come out of the closet 公开秘密；出柜<br>be closeted with 与…密谈 | She hung her coat in the **closet**. （她把外套挂在衣橱里。）<br>He was **closeted** with his lawyer for hours. （他和律师密谈了好几个小时。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：closet壁橱/隐秘的, cabinet陈列柜, wardrobe衣柜|
| | mistress | /ˈmɪstrəs/ | n. 女主人；情妇；<br>（英）女教师 | mistress of the house 家庭女主人<br>head mistress 女校长<br>school mistress 女教师 | The dog obeyed its **mistress** immediately. （那条狗立刻服从了它的女主人。）<br>The novel explores the scandal of a married man and his **mistress**. （这部小说探讨了一个已婚男人与情妇的丑闻。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：master(主人对应) / mistress女主人/情妇, mister先生|
| | accustom | /əˈkʌstəm/ | v. 使习惯，使适应 | accustom oneself to 使自己习惯于<br>be accustomed to (doing) sth 习惯于（做）某事<br>become/get accustomed to 逐渐习惯 | It took him a while to **accustom** himself to the new environment. （他花了一段时间来适应新环境。）<br>She is **accustomed** to getting up early. （她习惯早起。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：accustom使习惯, custom习俗, costume服装|
| | equator | /ɪˈkweɪtər/ | n. 赤道 | on the equator 在赤道上<br>near the equator 赤道附近<br>cross the equator 穿越赤道 | The climate near the **equator** is hot and humid all year round. （赤道附近的气候全年炎热潮湿。）<br>Ecuador is named after the Spanish word for **equator**. （厄瓜多尔以西班牙语中的"赤道"命名。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：equator赤道, equal相等的, equation方程|
| | overtake | /ˌoʊvərˈteɪk/ | v. 超过，赶上；<br>突然降临，压倒 | overtake a car 超车<br>be overtaken by 被…压倒/赶上<br>overtake sb on the inside 从内侧超越 | It's dangerous to **overtake** on a blind corner. （在盲角处超车很危险。）<br>He was **overtaken** by fear when he saw the shadow. （看到那个影子时，他被恐惧压倒。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：surpass(超越) / overtake超车/超越, take over接管|
| 2026-05-28 | bewilder | /bɪˈwɪldər/ | v. 使迷惑，使困惑，<br>使不知所措 | be bewildered by 被…弄糊涂<br>bewilder sb 使某人困惑<br>a bewildering array of 五花八门的 | The complex instructions **bewildered** the students. （复杂的说明让学生们不知所措。）<br>Shoppers are faced with a **bewildering** variety of choices. （消费者面对五花八门的选择，眼花缭乱。） |
| | intersection | /ˌɪntərˈsekʃn/ | n. 十字路口，交叉路口；<br>交点，交叉点 | at the intersection of 在…的交叉处<br>intersection point 交点<br>busy intersection 繁忙的路口 | Turn left at the next **intersection**. （在下一个十字路口左转。）<br>The research lies at the **intersection** of biology and computer science. （这项研究处于生物学与计算机科学的交汇点。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：crossroad(十字路口) / intersection交叉点, intersection vs junction|
| | quantify | /ˈkwɑːntɪfaɪ/ | v. 量化，<br>测定…的数量 | quantify the impact 量化影响<br>quantify the risk 量化风险<br>difficult to quantify 难以量化 | It's hard to **quantify** the impact of social media on mental health. （社交媒体对心理健康的影响很难量化。）<br>The researchers tried to **quantify** the economic benefits of the new policy. （研究人员试图量化新政策的经济效益。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：measure(量化) / quantify量化, quality质量, quantity数量|
| | considerable | /kənˈsɪdərəbl/ | adj. 相当大的，相当多的；<br>重要的，值得考虑的 | considerable amount/number 大量的<br>considerable influence 相当大的影响<br>considerable attention 相当的关注<br>considerable progress 显著的进步 | The project required a **considerable** amount of time and effort. （该项目需要大量的时间和精力。）<br>She has made **considerable** progress in her English studies. （她在英语学习上取得了显著的进步。）<br>The earthquake caused **considerable** damage to the city. （地震给该城市造成了相当大的破坏。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：significant(相当大) / considerable大量的, considerate体贴的(易混!)|
| | stall | /stɔːl/ | v. 拖延，推迟；<br>（发动机）熄火，停止运转<br>n. 货摊，摊位 | stall for time 拖延时间<br>stall the engine 使发动机熄火<br>market stall 集市摊位<br>stall sb off 搪塞某人 | The car **stalled** at the traffic light. （车在红绿灯处熄火了。）<br>He's just **stalling** — he doesn't want to give an answer. （他只是在拖延——他不想给出答案。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：booth(摊位) / stall stalls(摊位/熄火), still仍然|
| | wedge | /wedʒ/ | n. 楔子，三角木<br>v. 楔入，塞入；<br>挤进 | drive a wedge between 在…之间挑拨离间<br>wedge open 楔开，撬开<br>wedge oneself into 挤进 | He **wedged** the door open with a piece of wood. （他用一块木头把门楔开，让门保持开着。）<br>She **wedged** herself into the crowded subway car. （她挤进了拥挤的地铁车厢。）<br>Their disagreement drove a **wedge** between them. （他们的分歧在他们之间造成了隔阂。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：wedge楔子/挤入, edge边缘, hedge树篱|
| | weep | /wiːp/ | v. 哭泣，流泪；<br>（伤口）渗出液体 | weep over/for 为…哭泣<br>weep with joy 喜极而泣<br>weep bitterly 痛哭 | She **wept** when she heard the sad news. （她听到这个悲伤的消息时哭了起来。）<br>They **wept** tears of joy at their reunion. （他们在重逢时流下了喜悦的泪水。）<br>The wound **wept** for days before healing. （伤口渗液好几天才愈合。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：cry(哭泣) / weep哭泣(正式), wipe擦(拼写像)|
| | rejoice | /rɪˈdʒɔɪs/ | v. 欣喜，高兴；<br>感到喜悦 | rejoice in/at/over 因…而欣喜<br>rejoice to do sth 很高兴做某事<br>rejoice in sth 拥有（某种称号/特点） | We **rejoiced** at the good news. （我们为这个好消息感到欣喜。）<br>He **rejoiced** in his success. （他为自己的成功感到高兴。）<br>Let us **rejoice** together! （让我们一起欢庆吧！） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：celebrate(庆祝) / rejoice欣喜, rejoice vs joy(rejoice动词, joy名词)|
| | likewise | /ˈlaɪkwaɪz/ | adv. 同样地，类似地；<br>也，而且 | equally likewise 同样地<br>do likewise 照着做，同样做<br>"Likewise!" "我也是！" | She decided to go and I did **likewise**. （她决定去，我也同样去了。）<br>The food was excellent — **likewise**, the service was outstanding. （食物非常好——同样地，服务也极为出色。）<br>"Nice to meet you." "**Likewise**." （"很高兴认识你。""我也是。"） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：similarly(同样地) / likewise同样, likely可能的|
| | succession | /səkˈseʃn/ | n. 连续，一连串；<br>继承，继任 | a succession of 一连串的<br>in succession 连续地<br>succession to 对…的继承<br>right of succession 继承权 | He won the championship for the third year in **succession**. （他连续第三年获得冠军。）<br>A **succession** of warm days melted the snow. （一连串温暖的日子融化了积雪。）<br>The eldest son is first in **succession** to the throne. （长子是王位的第一继承人。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：sequence(连续) / succession连续/继承, success成功|
| | expel | /ɪkˈspel/ | v. 开除，驱逐；<br>排出（气体等） | expel sb from 把某人从…开除/驱逐<br>expel air/gas 排出空气/气体<br>be expelled from school 被学校开除 | He was **expelled** from school for cheating. （他因作弊被学校开除。）<br>The government **expelled** the foreign diplomat. （政府驱逐了该外国外交官。）<br>The lungs **expel** carbon dioxide when we breathe out. （我们呼气时，肺排出二氧化碳。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：eject(驱逐) / expel开除/驱逐, repel击退, excel擅长|
| | grief | /ɡriːf/ | n. 悲伤，悲痛；<br>伤心事 | come to grief 失败，遭难<br>grief over/at 因…而悲伤<br>good grief （感叹语）天哪！哎呀！<br>share one's grief 分担某人的悲痛 | She was overcome with **grief** at the loss of her mother. （她因失去母亲而悲痛不已。）<br>His plans came to **grief** when he lost his funding. （他失去资金后计划就失败了。）<br>Good **grief**! Is it really that late? （天哪！真的这么晚了吗？） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：sorrow(悲伤) / grief悲痛, brief简短的, relief Relief(反)|
| | dial | /ˈdaɪəl/ | n. 刻度盘，钟面；<br>拨号盘<br>v. 拨（电话号码） | dial the number 拨号<br>dial tone 拨号音<br>dial in 拨入（电话会议等）<br>clock dial 钟面 | Pick up the receiver and **dial** the number. （拿起听筒拨号。）<br>She **dialed** 911 immediately when she saw the fire. （她看到火灾后立刻拨打了911。）<br>The **dial** of the watch is luminous. （手表的表盘是夜光的。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dial拨号/表盘, dialog对话, dialect方言|
| | stitch | /stɪtʃ/ | n. （缝纫的）一针，针脚；<br>缝法，针法<br>v. 缝，缝合 | stitch up 缝合；缝补<br>in stitches 捧腹大笑<br>a stitch 突然的剧痛（尤指侧腹）<br>stitch sth together 把…缝合起来 | The doctor **stitched** up the wound carefully. （医生仔细地缝合了伤口。）<br>Her joke had us all in **stitches**. （她的笑话让我们都笑得前仰后合。）<br>She put a few **stitches** in the torn hem. （她在撕破的衣边缝了几针。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：sew(缝) / stitch缝合, stitch vs itch(缝vs痒)|
| | abuse | /əˈbjuːs/ (n.)<br>/əˈbjuːz/ (v.) | n. 滥用；虐待；辱骂<br>v. 滥用；虐待；辱骂 | drug abuse 药物滥用<br>child abuse 虐待儿童<br>abuse of power 滥用职权<br>verbal abuse 言语辱骂 | He was accused of **abuse** of power. （他被指控滥用职权。）<br>The prisoners suffered physical and emotional **abuse**. （囚犯们遭受了身体和情感上的虐待。）<br>She **abused** her position for personal gain. （她滥用职权谋取私利。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：mistreat(虐待) / abuse滥用/虐待, use使用, accuse指控|
| | debut | /deɪˈbjuː/ | n. 首次亮相，初次登台<br>v. 首次推出，初次登场 | make one's debut 首次亮相<br>debut album 首张专辑<br>debut performance 首演<br>debut match 首场比赛 | The band made their **debut** last year. （这支乐队去年首次亮相。）<br>She made her acting **debut** at the age of ten. （她十岁时首次登台表演。）<br>The company's new product will **debut** next month. （该公司的新产品将于下个月首次推出。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：debut首次亮相, debt债务(发音不同)|
| | hatch | /hætʃ/ | v. 孵出，孵化；<br>策划，密谋<br>n. （船上或飞机上的）舱口 | hatch a plan 策划一个计划<br>hatch out 孵出<br>hatch an idea 想出主意<br>escape hatch 逃生舱口 | The eggs **hatched** after three weeks. （蛋在三周后孵化了。）<br>They **hatched** a plan to rob the bank. （他们策划了一个抢银行的计划。）<br>Don't count your chickens before they're **hatched**. （别高兴得太早/别打如意算盘。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hatch孵化/策划, catch抓住, match匹配|
| | complaint | /kəmˈpleɪnt/ | n. 投诉，抱怨；<br>申诉；疾病 | make a complaint 投诉<br>file a complaint 提出申诉<br>complaint about 对…的抱怨<br>customer complaint 客户投诉 | She filed a **complaint** against the company. （她对该公司提出了投诉。）<br>He has a minor **complaint** — a sore throat. （他有个小毛病——喉咙痛。）<br>Our biggest **complaint** is the poor service. （我们最大的不满是服务质量差。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：grievance(投诉) / complaint投诉/抱怨, complain动词|
| | plea | /pliː/ | n. 恳求，请求；<br>辩护，借口 | plea for 恳求…<br>enter/make a plea 提出抗辩<br>guilty plea 认罪<br>on the plea of 以…为借口 | She made a **plea** for help. （她恳求帮助。）<br>He was absent on the **plea** of illness. （他以生病为借口缺席了。）<br>The defendant entered a **plea** of not guilty. （被告进行了无罪抗辩。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：appeal(恳求) / plea恳求/辩护, plead动词, please请|
| | rouse | /raʊz/ | v. 唤醒，弄醒；<br>激发，激起（情感） | rouse sb from 把某人从…唤醒<br>rouse sb to action 激励某人行动起来<br>rouse interest/anger 激起兴趣/愤怒<br>rouse oneself 振作起来 | The alarm **roused** me from a deep sleep. （闹钟把我从沉睡中唤醒。）<br>His speech **roused** the audience to action. （他的演讲激励了听众行动起来。）<br>It took a lot of effort to **rouse** myself to go to the gym. （我费了好大劲才振作起来去健身房。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：arouse(唤醒) / rouse唤起/唤醒, raise举起, rose玫瑰|
| | colony | /ˈkɑːləni/ | n. 殖民地；<br>（同类人的）聚居地，群体 | colonial adj. 殖民地的<br>colonist n. 殖民者<br>colonize v. 殖民；开拓殖民地<br>bee colony 蜂群<br>artists' colony 艺术家聚居地 | The country was once a British **colony**. （该国曾是英国殖民地。）<br>A **colony** of ants lives under the stone. （一群蚂蚁生活在石头下面。）<br>There is a large Chinese **colony** in this city. （这个城市有一个很大的华人聚居区。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：colony殖民地, colonial殖民的, colon冒号|
| | dye | /daɪ/ | n. 染料，染色<br>v. 给…染色 | hair dye 染发剂<br>dye sth red/blue 把某物染成红/蓝色<br>dyed-in-the-wool 根深蒂固的；彻头彻尾的 | She **dyed** her hair blonde. （她把头发染成了金色。）<br>The fabric takes **dye** well. （这种布料很好上色。）<br>He is a **dyed**-in-the-wool conservative. （他是个彻头彻尾的保守派。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：dye染色, die死亡(同音!)|
| | promising | /ˈprɑːmɪsɪŋ/ | adj. 有希望的，<br>有前途的，有潜力的 | promising future 有前途的未来<br>promising start 良好的开端<br>promising young talent 有前途的年轻人<br>look promising 看起来很有希望 | He is a **promising** young student. （他是一个有前途的年轻学生。）<br>The weather looks **promising** for tomorrow. （明天的天气看起来不错。）<br>The project got off to a **promising** start. （这个项目有了一个良好的开端。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：hopeful(有希望) / promising有前途, compromised妥协的(反义)|
| | champagne | /ʃæmˈpeɪn/ | n. 香槟酒 | champagne bottle 香槟瓶<br>champagne toast 香槟祝酒<br>champagne flute 香槟杯<br>champagne breakfast 香槟早餐 | They opened a bottle of **champagne** to celebrate. （他们开了一瓶香槟庆祝。）<br>We drank a **champagne** toast at the wedding. （我们在婚礼上喝香槟祝酒。）<br>Success tastes like **champagne**. （成功尝起来像香槟一样甜美。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：champagne香槟, champion冠军, campaign运动|
| | insure | /ɪnˈʃʊr/ | v. 给…投保，为…保险；<br>（美）确保（=ensure） | insure sth against 给某物投保…险<br>insure one's life 投保人寿保险<br>insure against 防范，预防 | You should **insure** your house against fire. （你应该为房子投保火险。）<br>Are you **insured** for medical expenses abroad? （你在国外就医有保险吗？）<br>Check your work to **insure** accuracy. （检查你的工作以确保准确性。） <br><br>🔍 **近义**：ensure(确保) / insure保险, ensure确保, assure使确信|
| 2026-06-07 | strap | /stræp/ | n. 带子，皮带<br>v. 用带子捆扎；<br>包扎 | shoulder strap 肩带<br>strap in 系好安全带<br>strap on 把…绑在…上 | He **strapped** the bag onto his back. （他把包捆在背上。）<br>Make sure your seatbelt is **strapped** in. （确保你的安全带系好了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：belt(腰带) / strip(条) / strap带子(可捆绑), belt腰带(服饰), strip狭长条|
| | inflict | /ɪnˈflɪkt/ | v. 使遭受（打击、痛苦等），<br>施加 | inflict sth on/upon sb 使某人遭受某事<br>inflict damage 造成损害<br>inflict punishment 施加惩罚 | The storm **inflicted** widespread damage. （暴风雨造成了大范围的破坏。）<br>Don't **inflict** your problems on me. （别把你的问题强加给我。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：impose(强加) / inflict施加痛苦/打击, impose强加(规则/观点)|
| | controversy | /ˈkɑːntrəvɜːrsi/ | n. 争论，争议，辩论 | arouse/cause controversy 引起争议<br>be in controversy 有争议<br>beyond controversy 无可争议<br>controversial adj. 有争议的 | The new policy sparked heated **controversy**. （新政策引发了激烈的争议。）<br>There is **controversy** over the safety of the drug. （关于该药物的安全性存在争议。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：dispute(争端) / controversy持久重大争议, argument口头争论|
| | shear | /ʃɪr/ | v. 剪（羊毛等）；<br>切断，剪切<br>n. 大剪刀 | shear off 剪掉，切断<br>sheep shearing 剪羊毛<br>be shorn of 被剥夺 | Farmers **shear** the sheep every spring. （农民每年春天给羊剪毛。）<br>The machine **sheared** the metal rod in two. （机器把金属棒剪成两段。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：cut(剪) / shear剪羊毛/剪切, sheer完全的/陡峭的(同音!)|
| | dual | /ˈduːəl/ | adj. 双重的，<br>两部分的 | dual purpose 双重目的<br>dual role 双重角色<br>dual citizenship 双重国籍<br>dual carriageway 双行道 | The bridge has a **dual** purpose — it carries both road and rail traffic. （这座桥有双重功能——同时承载公路和铁路交通。）<br>She holds **dual** citizenship in the US and Canada. （她拥有美加双重国籍。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：double(双倍) / dual强调双重属性, duel决斗(同音!)|
| | thrift | /θrɪft/ | n. 节俭，节约；<br>（美）储蓄机构 | thrift store 二手店，旧货店<br>thrift shop 廉价旧货店<br>practice thrift 厉行节约<br>thrifty adj. 节俭的 | **Thrift** is a virtue that should be cultivated. （节俭是应该培养的美德。）<br>She bought a vintage dress at a **thrift** store. （她在二手店买了一条复古裙子。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：frugality(节俭) / waste(反) / thrift节俭储蓄, thrive繁荣(拼写像)|
| | burglar | /ˈbɜːrɡlər/ | n. 入室窃贼，破门盗窃者 | burglar alarm 防盗报警器<br>burglarize v. 入室盗窃<br>burglary n. 入室盗窃罪 | The **burglar** broke in through the window. （窃贼破窗而入。）<br>They installed a **burglar** alarm to protect their home. （他们安装了防盗报警器来保护家。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：burglar入室窃贼, thief小偷(泛指), robber抢劫犯(持械)|
| | quarantine | /ˈkwɔːrəntiːn/ | n. 隔离期，检疫<br>v. 隔离，检疫 | in quarantine 在隔离中<br>quarantine period 隔离期<br>be quarantined for 被隔离…天 | The passengers were put under **quarantine** for two weeks. （乘客被隔离了两周。）<br>The dog was **quarantined** before entering the country. （那只狗在入境前接受了检疫隔离。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：isolation(隔离) / quarantine检疫隔离(特定期限), isolation泛指隔离孤立|
| | illiterate | /ɪˈlɪtərət/ | adj. 不识字的，文盲的；<br>（某领域）外行的 | functionally illiterate 功能性文盲<br>computer illiterate 电脑盲<br>illiteracy n. 文盲 | Many children in remote areas remain **illiterate**. （偏远地区的许多孩子仍然是文盲。）<br>He is musically **illiterate** — he can't read notes. （他是音乐盲——看不懂乐谱。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：uneducated(未受教育) / literate(反) / illiterate不识字, irrelevant不相关|
| | manuscript | /ˈmænjuskrɪpt/ | n. 手稿，原稿；<br>手写本 | submit a manuscript 提交手稿<br>in manuscript 以手稿形式<br>original manuscript 原稿 | The author sent the **manuscript** to the publisher. （作者把手稿寄给了出版商。）<br>Several pages of the original **manuscript** were missing. （原始手稿缺了几页。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：draft(草稿) / manuscript手稿/原稿, script剧本/脚本, prescription处方|
| 2026-06-07 | fume | /fjuːm/ | n.（浓烈或有害的）<br>烟，气，汽<br>v. 冒烟；<br>发怒，生气 | exhaust fumes 废气<br>fumes from traffic 交通废气<br>fume at/over/about 对…生气<br>be fuming 非常愤怒 | The room was filled with toxic **fumes**. （房间里充满了有毒气体。）<br>She sat there **fuming** about the delay. （她坐在那里为延误而生气。）<br>Car exhaust **fumes** are harmful to the environment. （汽车废气对环境有害。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：smoke(烟) / fume(有害气体/发怒), perfume(香味) / fury(愤怒)|
| | apparatus | /ˌæpəˈrætəs/ | n. 仪器，设备，装置；<br>器官；<br>机构，组织 | medical apparatus 医疗设备<br>breathing apparatus 呼吸器<br>the state apparatus 国家机器<br>laboratory apparatus 实验室设备 | The lab has state-of-the-art **apparatus**. （实验室拥有最先进的设备。）<br>The firefighter wore breathing **apparatus**. （消防员戴着呼吸器。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：equipment(设备) / appliance(家用电器) / apparatus专用仪器/设备(不可数), device小型装置(可数)|
| | salient | /ˈseɪliənt/ | adj. 显著的，突出的；<br>最重要的 | salient feature 显著特征<br>salient point 要点<br>salient aspect 突出方面<br>most salient 最显著的 | The **salient** feature of this report is its focus on sustainability. （这份报告的显著特点是对可持续性的关注。）<br>Let me summarize the **salient** points of the argument. （让我总结一下论点的要点。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：prominent(突出的) / striking(引人注目的) / salient突出且重要的, excellent优秀的|
| | plagiarism | /ˈpleɪdʒərɪzəm/ | n. 抄袭，剽窃 | commit plagiarism 剽窃<br>plagiarize v. 抄袭，剽窃<br>academic plagiarism 学术剽窃<br>plagiarism detector 查重软件 | The paper was rejected due to **plagiarism**. （该论文因剽窃被拒。）<br>He was accused of **plagiarism** for copying from online sources. （他因抄袭网络资源被指控剽窃。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：copying(抄袭) / plagiarism(学术剽窃), fraud(欺诈) / plagiary(古语剽窃)|
| | feudal | /ˈfjuːdl/ | adj. 封建的，<br>封建制度的 | feudal system 封建制度<br>feudal society 封建社会<br>feudal lord 封建领主<br>feudal era 封建时代 | The **feudal** system dominated medieval Europe. （封建制度主导了中世纪的欧洲。）<br>**Feudal** lords owned vast amounts of land. （封建领主拥有大量土地。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：medieval(中世纪的) / feudal(封建的), feud(世仇/长期不和)同源词|
| | memo | /ˈmemoʊ/ | n. 备忘录，<br>内部通知 | write a memo 写备忘录<br>interoffice memo 内部通知<br>memo pad 备忘录本<br>send a memo 发送备忘录 | The manager sent a **memo** about the new policy. （经理发了一封关于新政策的内部通知。）<br>I'll write a **memo** to remind everyone of the meeting. （我会写个备忘录提醒大家开会。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：note(笔记) / memorandum(正式备忘录,memo的完整形式) / memo(内部通知), memory(记忆)同源|
| | marital | /ˈmærɪtl/ | adj. 婚姻的，<br>夫妻的 | marital status 婚姻状况<br>marital problems 婚姻问题<br>marital happiness 婚姻幸福<br>marital breakdown 婚姻破裂 | Please indicate your **marital** status on the form. （请在表格上注明你的婚姻状况。）<br>They sought counseling for their **marital** problems. （他们为婚姻问题寻求咨询。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：matrimonial(婚姻的,较正式) / marital(婚姻的) / marry(结婚) / **易混**：marital vs martial(marital婚姻的, martial军事的! 拼写极像)|
| | denounce | /dɪˈnaʊns/ | v. 谴责，指责；<br>告发 | denounce sb/sth as 指责…为…<br>denounce a policy 谴责一项政策<br>denounce corruption 谴责腐败<br>be denounced by 被…谴责 | The government was **denounced** for its inaction. （政府因不作为而受到谴责。）<br>He **denounced** the proposal as immoral. （他谴责该提议是不道德的。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：condemn(谴责) / criticize(批评) / denounce公开谴责+告发, renounce宣布放弃(同源)|
| | cathedral | /kəˈθiːdrəl/ | n. 大教堂 | St. Paul's Cathedral 圣保罗大教堂<br>cathedral city 有大教堂的城市<br>cathedral ceiling 拱形天花板 | The **cathedral** dominates the city skyline. （大教堂矗立在城市天际线上。）<br>They visited the ancient **cathedral** during their trip. （他们在旅途中参观了那座古老的大教堂。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：church(教堂) / chapel(小教堂) / cathedral(大教堂,主教堂), Catholic(天主教的)同源|
| | subtract | /səbˈtrækt/ | v. 减去，扣除 | subtract A from B 从B中减去A<br>subtract numbers 做减法<br>subtract 10% 减去10% | **Subtract** 3 from 7 and you get 4. （7减3等于4。）<br>The cost of the meal was **subtracted** from his salary. （餐费从他的工资中扣除了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：deduct(扣除) / add(加,反义) / subtract减去(数学), deduct扣除(费用), distract分心(拼写像)|
| | fling | /flɪŋ/ | v. 猛投，用力扔；<br>猛地移动<br>n. 一时的放纵 | fling sth at/towards 把某物扔向…<br>fling the door open 猛地打开门<br>fling oneself into 投身于<br>have a fling 放纵一番 | He **flung** his bag onto the sofa. （他把包扔到沙发上。）<br>She **flung** herself into her work after the divorce. （离婚后她全身心投入到工作中。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：throw(扔) / toss(轻抛) / fling用力猛扔(含情绪), slang俚语(拼写像)|
| | feeble | /ˈfiːbl/ | adj. 虚弱的，无力的；<br>无效的，站不住脚的 | feeble voice 微弱的声音<br>feeble attempt 无力的尝试<br>feeble excuse 站不住脚的借口<br>feeble light 微弱的光 | She made a **feeble** attempt to defend herself. （她做了一个无力的自我辩护。）<br>His argument was **feeble** and unconvincing. （他的论点站不住脚，没有说服力。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：weak(弱的) / frail(脆弱的) / feeble虚弱+无力(含贬义), fiber纤维(拼写像)|
| | mould | /moʊld/ | n. 霉，霉菌；<br>模子，模具；<br>（人的）性格，气质<br>v.（使）发霉；<br>塑造，影响 | mouldy adj. 发霉的<br>break the mould 打破常规<br>be cast in the same mould 一个模子刻出来的<br>mould sb/sth into 把…塑造成 | There was green **mould** on the bread. （面包上长了绿霉。）<br>Parents help **mould** their children's character. （父母帮助塑造孩子的性格。）<br>He broke the **mould** with his innovative approach. （他以创新的方法打破了常规。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：shape(塑造) / mold(美式拼写) / mould英式拼写, mode模式(同源), mound土堆(拼写像)|
| | patch | /pætʃ/ | n. 补丁，补片；<br>小块，小片<br>v. 修补，缝补 | patch up 修补；和解<br>eye patch 眼罩<br>a patch of 一小片…<br>patch things up 修复关系 | He put a **patch** on the hole in his jeans. （他在牛仔裤的破洞上打了个补丁。）<br>They managed to **patch** up their differences. （他们设法弥合了分歧。）<br>A **patch** of ice on the road caused the accident. （路上的一小片冰导致了事故。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：repair(修理) / mend(缝补) / patch打补丁/临时修补, match匹配(拼写像)|
| | condense | /kənˈdens/ | v. 压缩，浓缩；<br>凝缩，精简 | condense sth into 把…浓缩/精简成<br>condensed milk 炼乳<br>condense a report 精简报告<br>condense into steam 凝结成水蒸气 | He **condensed** the speech into five minutes. （他把演讲压缩到五分钟。）<br>Steam **condenses** into water when it cools. （蒸汽冷却时凝结成水。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：compress(压缩) / dilute(稀释,反义) / condense浓缩+精简, dense密集的(同根)|
| | dilute | /daɪˈluːt/ | v. 稀释，冲淡；<br>削弱，降低 | dilute sth with 用…稀释<br>dilute the effect 削弱效果<br>diluted solution 稀释溶液<br>dilute the value 降低价值 | You can **dilute** the juice with water. （你可以用水稀释果汁。）<br>The quality of the product was **diluted** by cost-cutting. （产品质量因削减成本而下降。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：water down(稀释/淡化) / condense(浓缩,反义) / dilute稀释+削弱, dilute vs delute(古语冲洗,注意拼写)|
| | deputy | /ˈdepjəti/ | n. 副手，副职；<br>代理人 | deputy director 副主任/副局长<br>deputy manager 副经理<br>act as a deputy 代理<br>deputy sheriff 副警长 | He was appointed **deputy** director of the institute. （他被任命为研究所副所长。）<br>I'll send a **deputy** to attend the meeting. （我会派代理人参加会议。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：substitute(替代者) / assistant(助理) / deputy副职(有实权), delegate代表(同源), department部门|
| | precaution | /prɪˈkɔːʃn/ | n. 预防措施，<br>防备 | take precautions 采取预防措施<br>as a precaution 作为预防<br>safety precaution 安全措施<br>precautionary adj. 预防的 | We must take all necessary **precautions** against fire. （我们必须采取一切必要的防火措施。）<br>As a **precaution**, lock all windows before leaving. （作为预防，离开前锁好所有窗户。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：prevention(预防) / caution(谨慎) / precaution预防措施, caution小心谨慎(同根)|
| | chimney | /ˈtʃɪmni/ | n. 烟囱，<br>烟道 | chimney sweep 扫烟囱的人<br>chimney stack 烟囱体<br>factory chimney 工厂烟囱<br>smoke like a chimney 烟瘾极大 | Smoke rose from the factory **chimneys**. （烟从工厂的烟囱中升起。）<br>The **chimney** needs cleaning before winter. （烟囱在冬天前需要清理。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：flue(烟道) / funnel(漏斗/烟囱) / chimney(烟囱,建筑物外部), chin下巴(拼写像)|
| | stack | /stæk/ | n. 堆，一摞；<br>大量<br>v. 堆放，堆积 | a stack of 一堆/一摞…<br>stack up 堆积；累积<br>stack sth against 使对…不利<br>stack of books 一摞书 | There was a **stack** of papers on his desk. （他桌上有一摞文件。）<br>The shelves were **stacked** with books. （书架上堆满了书。）<br>The odds are **stacked** against us. （形势对我们不利。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：pile(堆) / heap(杂乱堆) / stack整齐堆放的一摞/一堆, stock库存(同音易混)|
| | dwarf | /dwɔːrf/ | n. 矮人，侏儒<br>v. 使相形见绌，<br>使显得矮小 | dwarf star 矮星<br>white dwarf 白矮星<br>be dwarfed by 在…面前显得渺小 | The new skyscraper **dwarfs** all the surrounding buildings. （新建的摩天大楼使用围建筑相形见绌。）<br>He was **dwarfed** by the enormous statue. （他在巨大的雕像面前显得很矮小。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：overshadow(使黯然失色) / dwarf(使相形见绌,强调体积对比), dwarf vs midget(中性词midget现多有冒犯,勿用)|
| | wage | /weɪdʒ/ | n.（常 pl. wages）工资，薪水<br>v. 发动，进行（战争/运动） | minimum wage 最低工资<br>wage war on/against 对…发动战争<br>wage a campaign 发起一场运动<br>hourly wage 时薪 | The government is **waging** a war against corruption. （政府正在进行一场反腐斗争。）<br>They **waged** a long campaign for equal rights. （他们为平等权利发起了长期运动。）<br>Workers demanded higher **wages**. （工人要求提高工资。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：salary(月薪) / wage(周薪/时薪,常用复数) / salary白领月薪, wage蓝领周薪, engage从事(同根)|
| | invaluable | /ɪnˈvæljuəbl/ | adj. 非常宝贵的，<br>极其珍贵的 | invaluable experience 宝贵的经验<br>invaluable asset 宝贵的财富<br>invaluable resource 宝贵的资源<br>be invaluable to/for 对…极其宝贵 | Your advice has been **invaluable** to me. （你的建议对我极其宝贵。）<br>Her **invaluable** contribution made the project a success. （她宝贵的贡献使项目取得成功。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：priceless(无价的) / valuable(有价值的) / **易混**：invaluable ≠ not valuable(其实是宝贵的!), valueless无价值的(才是反义)|
| | controversial | /ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃl/ | adj. 有争议的，<br>引起争论的 | controversial issue 有争议的问题<br>controversial decision 有争议的决定<br>highly controversial 极具争议的<br>controversial figure 有争议的人物 | The new policy is highly **controversial**. （新政策极具争议。）<br>He is a **controversial** figure in the art world. （他是艺术界一个有争议的人物。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：debatable(可争论的) / contentious(有争议的) / controversial(多指引起公开争论), controversy名词形式(已在单词本中)|
| | endow | /ɪnˈdaʊ/ | v. 资助，捐赠；<br>赋予（品质等） | endow sb/sth with 赋予…（品质/能力）<br>be endowed with 天生具有…<br>endow a scholarship 捐赠奖学金<br>endowment n. 捐赠；天赋 | She is **endowed** with great intelligence. （她天生聪慧。）<br>The university was **endowed** by a wealthy businessman. （这所大学由一位富商捐资建立。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：donate(捐赠) / grant(授予) / **易混**：endow(捐赠/赋予, 搭配with) vs donate(捐赠物品/钱, 搭配to)|
| | conquer | /ˈkɑːŋkər/ | v. 征服，攻克；<br>克服（困难/恐惧） | conquer a country 征服一个国家<br>conquer one's fears 克服恐惧<br>conquer difficulties 克服困难<br>divide and conquer 分而治之 | The Romans **conquered** most of Europe. （罗马人征服了欧洲大部分地区。）<br>She managed to **conquer** her fear of heights. （她设法克服了恐高症。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：defeat(击败) / overcome(克服) / **易混**：conquer(征服+克服) vs vanquish(彻底击溃,文学用语)|
| | fountain | /ˈfaʊntn/ | n. 喷泉，泉水；<br>源泉，根源 | water fountain 饮水器<br>fountain of youth 青春之泉<br>fountain of knowledge 知识的源泉<br>drinking fountain 直饮水机 | The **fountain** in the park is beautiful at night. （公园里的喷泉在夜晚很美。）<br>The library is a **fountain** of knowledge. （图书馆是知识的源泉。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：spring(泉水/泉源) / source(来源) / **易混**：fountain(人工喷泉+比喻源泉) vs spring(天然泉+弹簧)|
| | temperament | /ˈtemprəmənt/ | n. 气质，性情，<br>性格 | artistic temperament 艺术气质<br>have a cheerful temperament 性情开朗<br>by temperament 从性格上<br>temperamental adj. 喜怒无常的 | She has an artistic **temperament**. （她有着艺术家的气质。）<br>He is calm by **temperament**. （他生性沉着冷静。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：disposition(性情) / personality(个性) / **易混**：temperament(先天气质) vs temper(脾气/怒火,temperament的简化) vs temperature(温度,拼写像)|
| | haste | /heɪst/ | n. 匆忙，急忙 | in haste 匆忙地<br>make haste 赶快<br>haste makes waste 欲速则不达<br>hasten v. 加快，促进 | More **haste**, less speed. （欲速则不达。）<br>In their **haste** to leave, they forgot the tickets. （匆忙离开时，他们忘了带票。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：rush(匆忙) / hurry(急忙) / **易混**：haste(名词"匆忙") vs hasten(动词"加快") vs hasty(形容词"仓促的")|
| | bribe | /braɪb/ | n. 贿赂<br>v. 向…行贿 | take/accept a bribe 收受贿赂<br>offer a bribe 行贿<br>bribe sb into doing 贿赂某人做某事<br>bribery n. 贿赂行为 | The official was accused of taking **bribes**. （该官员被指控受贿。）<br>He tried to **bribe** the guard into letting him in. （他试图贿赂警卫让他进去。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：payoff(贿金) / corrupt(腐败) / bribe(行贿行为), bride新娘(拼写差一个字母!)|
| | tumour | /ˈtuːmər/ | n. 肿瘤，肿块<br>（美式拼写 tumor） | brain tumour 脑肿瘤<br>malignant tumour 恶性肿瘤<br>benign tumour 良性肿瘤<br>remove a tumour 切除肿瘤 | Doctors found a **tumour** in his lung. （医生在他的肺部发现了一个肿瘤。）<br>The **tumour** was successfully removed by surgery. （肿瘤通过手术成功切除了。）<br><br>🔍 **易混**：tumour(肿瘤) vs rumor(谣言,拼写像) / tumor美式拼写, tumour英式拼写|
| | frost | /frɔːst/ | n. 霜，霜冻；<br>严寒天气<br>v. 结霜 | frosty adj. 霜冻的；冷淡的<br>ground frost 地面霜冻<br>frost damage 霜冻损害<br>frost over/up 结霜 | The car windows were covered with **frost**. （车窗上结满了霜。）<br>There was a hard **frost** last night. （昨晚有严重霜冻。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：ice(冰) / freeze(冰冻) / **易混**：frost(霜/霜冻) vs frost vs fog(霜vs雾) / 词链：frost(霜) → frosty(霜冻的/冷淡的) → defrost(除霜)|
| | auxiliary | /ɔːɡˈzɪliəri/ | adj. 辅助的，<br>备用的<br>n. 辅助人员，<br>助动词 | auxiliary verb 助动词<br>auxiliary equipment 辅助设备<br>auxiliary staff 辅助人员<br>auxiliary power 备用电源 | The hospital has an **auxiliary** power supply. （医院有备用电源。）<br>He works as an **auxiliary** nurse. （他是一名助理护士。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：subsidiary(辅助的/附属的) / supplementary(补充的) / **易混**：auxiliary(辅助/备用) vs subsidiary(子公司/附属) vs auxiliary verb(助动词,语法术语)|
| | advisable | /ədˈvaɪzəbl/ | adj. 明智的，<br>可取的，适当的 | it is advisable to do sth 做…是明智的<br>highly advisable 非常可取<br>advisable course of action 明智的做法<br>advise v. 建议，劝告 | It is **advisable** to book tickets in advance. （提前订票是明智的。）<br>Regular exercise is **advisable** for good health. （经常锻炼对健康是可取的。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：recommended(被推荐的) / wise(明智的) / **易混**：advisable(明智的,it作形式主语) vs advisory(咨询的/顾问的) vs advice(建议,名词)|
| | spectator | /ˈspekteɪtər/ | n.（体育比赛或<br>其他活动的）<br>观众，旁观者 | spectator sport 观赏性运动<br>spectators cheer 观众欢呼<br>mere spectator 仅仅是旁观者<br>spectate v. 观看 | The stadium can hold 80,000 **spectators**. （这个体育场可容纳8万名观众。）<br>He was a **spectator** at the event. （他是该活动的旁观者。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：audience(观众/听众) / viewer(观众) / **易混**：spectator(体育/活动观众) vs audience(演出/电影观众) vs eyewitness(目击者) · 同根词：inspect(检查), prospect(前景), respect(尊重)|
| | stain | /steɪn/ | n. 污点，污渍<br>v. 弄脏，染色；<br>玷污 | remove a stain 去除污渍<br>stainless steel 不锈钢<br>stain sb's reputation 玷污某人名誉<br>stained glass 彩色玻璃 | Red wine can leave a permanent **stain**. （红酒会留下永久性污渍。）<br>His reputation was **stained** by the scandal. （他的名誉被丑闻玷污了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：spot(污点) / mark(痕迹) / **易混**：stain(污渍/玷污) vs stain vs steel(钢) · **stainless** = stain(污点)+less(无)=不锈的, 也指"没有污点的"|
| 2026-06-08 | strife | /straɪf/ | n. 冲突，争斗，纷争 | internal strife 内讧<br>political strife 政治斗争<br>civil strife 内乱 | The country was torn apart by years of **strife**. （这个国家被连年的内乱撕裂。）<br>Family **strife** can have a lasting impact on children. （家庭纷争会对孩子产生持久的影响。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：conflict（泛指冲突，更正式）· **反义**：harmony（和谐）· **易混**：strife vs struggle（strife 指纷争/冲突状态，struggle 指挣扎/奋斗过程）|
| 2026-06-09 | heir (第2次) | /er/ | n. 继承人，后嗣；<br>接班人 | heir to the throne 王位继承人<br>legal heir 法定继承人<br>fall heir to 继承…<br>heiress n. 女继承人 | He is the sole **heir** to the family business. （他是家族企业的唯一继承人。）<br>The young prince was declared **heir** to the throne at birth. （这位年轻王子一出生就被宣布为王位继承人。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：successor（接班人/继任者，更正式）· **反义**：ancestor（祖先）· **易混**：heir（继承人，h不发音）vs hair（头发，同音词）vs heritage（遗产/传统，名词）|
| | impart | /ɪmˈpɑːrt/ | v. 传授，告知；<br>赋予，给予（品质/风味） | impart knowledge 传授知识<br>impart wisdom 传授智慧<br>impart sth to sb/sth 将…传授/赋予…<br>impart a flavor 赋予风味 | A good teacher can **impart** knowledge effectively. （好老师能有效地传授知识。）<br>The spices **impart** a unique flavor to the dish. （这些香料给菜肴赋予了独特的风味。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：convey（传达）/ transmit（传递）/ bestow（赋予）· **易混**：impart（传授/赋予）vs import（进口）vs impact（影响）|
| | ministry | /ˈmɪnɪstri/ | n.（政府的）部；<br>牧师职位，神职 | Ministry of Education 教育部<br>Ministry of Finance 财政部<br>enter the ministry 担任牧师 | The **Ministry** of Health issued new guidelines. （卫生部发布了新的指导方针。）<br>He decided to enter the **ministry** after college. （他大学毕业后决定当牧师。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：department（部门）/ bureau（局）· **易混**：ministry（政府的"部"）vs minister（部长/牧师）vs administer（管理）|
| | reservoir (第2次) | /ˈrezərvwɑːr/ | n. 水库，蓄水池；<br>（知识等的）储藏 | a reservoir of 大量的（知识/资源等）<br>water reservoir 水库<br>oil reservoir 油层，储油层 | The **reservoir** supplies water to the entire city. （这座水库为整个城市供水。）<br>She has a **reservoir** of knowledge about ancient history. （她拥有丰富的古代史知识储备。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：reservoir水库, reserve储备保护区|
| | summit | /ˈsʌmɪt/ | n. 山顶，顶峰；<br>峰会，最高会议 | summit meeting 峰会<br>reach the summit 登顶<br>summit of one's career 事业巅峰<br>G20 summit G20峰会 | They finally reached the **summit** of Mount Everest. （他们终于登上了珠穆朗玛峰顶。）<br>The **summit** was attended by leaders from 20 countries. （20个国家的领导人出席了此次峰会。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：peak（山顶/峰值）/ top（顶端）· **易混**：summit（峰会/山顶）vs submit（提交，拼写差一个字母）|
| | enquiry | /ɪnˈkwaɪəri/ | n. 询问，查询；<br>调查（= inquiry） | make an enquiry 进行查询<br>enquiry desk 问询处<br>preliminary enquiry 初步调查<br>line of enquiry 调查方向 | I'd like to make an **enquiry** about the train schedule. （我想查询一下火车时刻表。）<br>The police have launched an **enquiry** into the incident. （警方已对该事件展开调查。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：inquiry（查询/调查，美式更常用）/ investigation（正式调查）· **易混**：enquiry（英式拼写询问）vs inquiry（美式拼写，同义可互换）|
| | consent | /kənˈsent/ | n. 同意，许可<br>v. 同意，允许 | give consent 给予同意<br>without consent 未经许可<br>consent to (doing) sth 同意（做）某事<br>written consent 书面同意 | You cannot use the data without the user's **consent**. （未经用户同意，你不能使用这些数据。）<br>He reluctantly **consented** to the surgery. （他勉强同意了手术。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：agree（同意，动作）/ permission（许可，名词）· **易混**：consent（同意/许可）vs consensus（共识）vs resent（怨恨）|
| | albeit | /ɔːlˈbiːɪt/ | conj. 虽然，尽管 | albeit reluctantly 尽管不情愿<br>albeit briefly 尽管短暂<br>albeit at a cost 尽管有代价 | He accepted the offer, **albeit** with some hesitation. （他接受了提议，尽管有些犹豫。）<br>The plan was successful, **albeit** at a high cost. （计划成功了，尽管代价高昂。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：although（虽然）/ even though（即使）· **易混**：albeit（虽然，正式书面语，后接短语非从句）vs although（虽然，接从句）|
| | denounce (第2次) | /dɪˈnaʊns/ | v. 谴责，指责；<br>告发 | denounce sb/sth as 指责…为…<br>denounce a policy 谴责一项政策<br>denounce corruption 谴责腐败<br>be denounced by 被…谴责 | The government was **denounced** for its inaction. （政府因不作为而受到谴责。）<br>He **denounced** the proposal as immoral. （他谴责该提议是不道德的。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：condemn（谴责）/ criticize（批评）· **易混**：denounce（公开谴责+告发）vs renounce（宣布放弃，同源）|
| | fling (第2次) | /flɪŋ/ | v. 猛投，用力扔；<br>猛地移动<br>n. 一时的放纵 | fling sth at/towards 把某物扔向…<br>fling the door open 猛地打开门<br>fling oneself into 投身于<br>have a fling 放纵一番 | He **flung** his bag onto the sofa. （他把包扔到沙发上。）<br>She **flung** herself into her work after the divorce. （离婚后她全身心投入到工作中。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：throw（扔）/ toss（轻抛）· **易混**：fling（用力猛扔，含情绪）vs slang（俚语，拼写像）|
| | salient (第2次) | /ˈseɪliənt/ | adj. 显著的，突出的；<br>最重要的 | salient feature 显著特征<br>salient point 要点<br>salient aspect 突出方面<br>most salient 最显著的 | The **salient** feature of this report is its focus on sustainability. （这份报告的显著特点是对可持续性的关注。）<br>Let me summarize the **salient** points of the argument. （让我总结一下论点的要点。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：prominent（突出的）/ striking（引人注目的）· **易混**：salient（突出且重要的）vs excellent（优秀的）|
| | surplus (第2次) | /ˈsɜːrpləs/ | n. 过剩，剩余；盈余<br>adj. 多余的，过剩的 | trade surplus 贸易顺差<br>budget surplus 预算盈余<br>surplus value 剩余价值 | The country has a large trade **surplus** with its neighbors. （该国对邻国有巨额的贸易顺差。）<br>Any **surplus** funds will be invested in new projects. （任何多余的资金都将投入新项目。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：excess（超额/过度）· **反义**：deficiency/shortage · **易混**：surplus（偏"剩余/盈余"）vs excess（偏"超额/过度"）|
| | census | /ˈsensəs/ | n. 人口普查，统计调查 | national census 全国人口普查<br>carry out a census 进行普查<br>census data 普查数据<br>census report 普查报告 | The national **census** is conducted every ten years. （全国人口普查每十年进行一次。）<br>According to the **census**, the population has grown by 5%. （根据普查数据，人口增长了5%。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：survey（调查）/ statistics（统计）· **易混**：census（人口普查/官方统计）vs consensus（共识）vs senses（感官）|
| | divine | /dɪˈvaɪn/ | adj. 神的，神圣的；<br>极好的<br>v. 猜测，占卜 | divine power 神力<br>divine intervention 神的干预<br>divine inspiration 天赐灵感<br>divine the future 预测未来 | Many ancient cultures believed in **divine** power. （许多古代文化相信神力。）<br>It's hard to **divine** what the future holds. （很难预测未来会发生什么。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：sacred（神圣的）/ holy（神圣的）· **易混**：divine（神的神圣的）vs divide（分开）vs divine right（神授之权）|
| | hospitality (第2次) | /ˌhɑːspɪˈtæləti/ | n. 好客，殷勤；<br>（款待客人的）食物饮料 | hospitality industry 酒店餐饮业<br>hospitality suite 迎宾套房<br>show hospitality 表示殷勤好客 | Thank you for your warm **hospitality**. （感谢您的热情款待。）<br>She works in the **hospitality** industry. （她在酒店餐饮行业工作。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：hospitality（热情好客）· **易混**：hospitality vs hospital（医院）|

| | pit | /pɪt/ | n. 坑，矿井；<br>果核<br>v. 使凹陷；去核 | pit against 使对抗<br>bottomless pit 无底洞<br>armpit 腋窝<br>pit of the stomach 心窝 | The miners worked deep in the **pit**. （矿工们在矿井深处工作。）<br>He was **pitted** against the champion in the first round. （他在第一轮就被安排与冠军对决。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：hole（洞）/ mine（矿）· **易混**：pit（坑/矿井）vs pitfall（陷阱/隐患）vs pitch（音调/投掷）|
| | controversial (第2次) | /ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃl/ | adj. 有争议的，<br>引起争论的 | controversial issue 有争议的问题<br>controversial decision 有争议的决定<br>highly controversial 极具争议的<br>controversial figure 有争议的人物 | The new policy is highly **controversial**. （新政策极具争议。）<br>He is a **controversial** figure in the art world. （他是艺术界一个有争议的人物。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：debatable（可争论的）/ contentious（有争议的）· **易混**：controversial（引起公开争论）vs controversy（名词形式）|

| | strap (第2次) | /stræp/ | n. 带子，皮带<br>v. 用带子捆扎；<br>包扎 | shoulder strap 肩带<br>strap in 系好安全带<br>strap on 把…绑在…上 | He **strapped** the bag onto his back. （他把包捆在背上。）<br>Make sure your seatbelt is **strapped** in. （确保你的安全带系好了。）<br><br>🔍 **近义**：belt（腰带）/ strip（条）· **易混**：strap（带子，可捆绑）vs strip（狭长条）vs stripe（条纹）|
